//! Database-authoritative release history and explicit dormant pruning.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#4-explicit-pruning-and-audit — no implicit collector or unaudited data loss.
use super::*;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct DormantItem {
    /// Stable cursor; column/index names may coincide.
    pub key: String,
    pub path: String,
    pub kind: String,
    pub action: String,
    pub rows: u64,
    pub bytes: u64,
    pub asset_bytes: u64,
    /// Internal audit distinction: zero standalone cells may still be covered by a dormant row.
    #[serde(skip)]
    pub(crate) definition_only: bool,
    pub sample_ids: Vec<String>,
    #[serde(default,skip_serializing_if="Option::is_none")]
    pub before_rows:Option<u64>,
    #[serde(default,skip_serializing_if="Option::is_none")]
    pub after_rows:Option<u64>,
    #[serde(default,skip_serializing_if="Option::is_none")]
    pub restored_rows:Option<u64>,
    #[serde(default,skip_serializing_if="Option::is_none")]
    pub before_sample_ids:Option<Vec<String>>,
}

impl DormantItem {
    pub fn unit(&self) -> &'static str {
        match (self.kind.as_str(), self.rows) { ("column", 1) => "cell", ("column", _) => "cells", ("index", 1) => "entry", ("index", _) => "entries", (_, 1) => "row", _ => "rows" }
    }
}

impl Store {
    /// Values presently in active rows, independently of table population or other columns.
    pub fn rollback_incompatible_values(&self, current: &Schema, target: &Schema, name: &str, column: &str) -> Result<(u64, Vec<String>), RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.authority_transaction()?;
        let table = &target.tables[name];
        let kind = &table.columns[column];
        if table.engine_field(column) || table.maintained.contains_key(column) { return Ok((0, Vec::new())); }
        let mut count = 0;
        let mut samples = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
        let mut statement = tx.conn().prepare("SELECT data FROM r WHERE t=(SELECT num FROM _catalog WHERE name=?1) ORDER BY id")?;
        for data in statement.query_map([name], |r| r.get::<_, String>(0))? {
            let mut row: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data?).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
            crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, &current.tables[name]);
            if let Ok(value) = row.field(column)
                && super::narrowed::incompatible_column(&tx, table, name, column, kind, value)? {
                count += 1;
                if samples.len() < 5 {
                    let text = match value { Value::String(s) => format!("'{}'", s.replace('\\', "\\\\").replace('\'', "\\'").replace('\n', "\\n").replace('\r', "\\r")), _ => canonical_json(value) };
                    samples.insert(super::adopt_refusals::bounded(&text));
                }
            }
        }
        Ok((count, samples.into_iter().collect()))
    }
}

fn sql_number(n: u64) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError> {
    i64::try_from(n).map_err(|_| {
        RuntimeError::Generation("release counter exceeds SQLite integer range".into())
    })
}

pub(super) fn present(conn: &Connection) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    Ok(conn.query_row(
        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE type='table' AND name='_dormant_tables')",
        [],
        |r| r.get(0),
    )?)
}
pub(crate) fn inventory(
    conn: &Connection,
    after: Option<&str>,
    limit: usize,
) -> Result<Vec<DormantItem>, RuntimeError> {
    if !present(conn)? {
        return Ok(Vec::new());
    }
    let narrowed = if super::narrowed::present(conn)? {
        " UNION ALL SELECT c.name,'rows',c.name,NULL FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=n.t GROUP BY n.t "
    } else {""};
    let query=format!("SELECT path,kind,table_name,column_name FROM (
        SELECT table_name AS path,'table' AS kind,table_name,NULL AS column_name FROM _dormant_tables
        UNION ALL SELECT table_name||'.'||column_name,'column',table_name,column_name FROM _dormant_columns c WHERE table_name NOT IN (SELECT table_name FROM _dormant_tables) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values v JOIN _catalog t ON t.num=v.t WHERE t.name=c.table_name AND v.column_name=c.column_name)
        UNION ALL SELECT table_name||'.'||index_name,'index',table_name,index_name FROM _dormant_indexes {narrowed})
        WHERE (?1 IS NULL OR kind||':'||path>?1) ORDER BY kind||':'||path LIMIT ?2");
    let mut statement=conn.prepare(&query)?;
    let items: Vec<(String, String, String, Option<String>)> = statement
        .query_map(
            params![after, i64::try_from(limit).unwrap_or(i64::MAX)],
            |r| Ok((r.get(0)?, r.get(1)?, r.get(2)?, r.get(3)?)),
        )?
        .collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for (path, kind, name, column) in items {
        let t: Option<i64> = conn
            .query_row("SELECT num FROM _catalog WHERE name=?1", [&name], |r| {
                r.get(0)
            })
            .optional()?;
        let Some(t) = t else { continue };
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#11-wave-11-factual-release-narration — dormant row images cover their retained cells; standalone cells have active owners.
        let (mut count,mut bytes):(u64,u64)=match kind.as_str(){
            "table"=>conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*),COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(id)+octet_length(data)),0) FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1",[t],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,i64>(0)? as u64,r.get::<_,i64>(1)? as u64)))?,
            "rows"=>conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*),COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(id)+octet_length(data)),0) FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1",[t],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,i64>(0)? as u64,r.get::<_,i64>(1)? as u64)))?,
            "column"=>conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*),COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(id)+octet_length(data)),0) FROM _dormant_values v WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id)",params![t,column],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,i64>(0)? as u64,r.get::<_,i64>(1)? as u64)))?,
            _=>conn.query_row("SELECT COUNT(*),0 FROM (SELECT id FROM r WHERE t=?1 UNION SELECT id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1)",[t],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,i64>(0)? as u64,r.get::<_,i64>(1)? as u64)))?,
        };
        if matches!(kind.as_str(), "table" | "rows") {
            let images = if kind == "table" { "_dormant_rows" } else { "_dormant_narrow_rows" };
            bytes += conn.query_row(&format!("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(octet_length(v.id)+octet_length(v.data)),0) FROM _dormant_values v WHERE v.t=?1 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM {images} d WHERE d.t=v.t AND d.id=v.id)"), [t], |r| r.get::<_, i64>(0))? as u64;
        }
        let query = match kind.as_str() {
            "rows" => "SELECT id FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND ?2 IS NULL ORDER BY id LIMIT 5",
            "table" => "SELECT id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 AND ?2 IS NULL ORDER BY id LIMIT 5",
            "column" => {
                "SELECT id FROM _dormant_values v WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id) ORDER BY id LIMIT 5"
            }
            _ => {
                "SELECT id FROM (SELECT id FROM r WHERE t=?1 UNION SELECT id FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1) WHERE ?2 IS NOT NULL ORDER BY id LIMIT 5"
            }
        };
        let mut ids = conn
            .prepare(query)?
            .query_map(params![t, column], |r| r.get::<_, String>(0))?
            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?
            .into_iter()
            .map(|id| super::adopt_refusals::bounded(&id))
            .collect();
        if kind == "index" {
            (count, ids) = index_entries(conn, t, &name, column.as_deref().unwrap())?;
        }
        let asset_bytes = if kind == "index" {
            0
        } else {
            let selector=match kind.as_str(){
                "rows"=>"EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=?3 AND n.id=_assets.home_id)",
                "column"=>"EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values v JOIN r ON r.t=v.t AND r.id=v.id WHERE v.t=?3 AND v.id=_assets.home_id AND v.column_name=_assets.home_column)",
                _=>"EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_rows d WHERE d.t=?3 AND d.id=_assets.home_id)",
            };
            conn.query_row(&format!("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(bytes),0) FROM _assets WHERE home_table=?1 AND (?2 IS NULL OR home_column=?2) AND released_at IS NULL AND {selector}"),params![name,if kind=="table" || kind=="rows" {None}else{column.as_deref()},t],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0).map(|n|n as u64))?
        };
        let definition_only = if kind == "column" {
            !conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND column_name=?2)", params![t, column], |r| r.get::<_, bool>(0))?
        } else { count == 0 };
        out.push(DormantItem {
            key: format!("{kind}:{path}"),
            path,
            kind,
            action: "dormant".into(),
            rows: count,
            bytes,
            asset_bytes,
            definition_only,
            sample_ids: ids,
            before_rows:None,after_rows:None,restored_rows:None,before_sample_ids:None,
        });
    }
    Ok(out)
}

/// Retained definitions represent logical entries, rebuilt on restoration.
/// A words index has zero or many entries per image; a row count is not its size.
fn index_entries(conn: &Connection, t: i64, table: &str, index: &str) -> Result<(u64, Vec<String>), RuntimeError> {
    let definition: String = conn.query_row("SELECT definition FROM _dormant_indexes WHERE table_name=?1 AND index_name=?2", params![table, index], |r| r.get(0))?;
    let index: crate::schema::Index = serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
    let schema: Option<String> = conn.query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation=(SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key='generation')", [], |r| r.get(0)).optional()?;
    let schema: Option<Schema> = schema.map(|s| serde_json::from_str(&s).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))).transpose()?;
    let query = if super::narrowed::present(conn)? {
        "SELECT id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 UNION ALL SELECT id,data FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 UNION ALL SELECT id,data FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 ORDER BY id"
    } else { "SELECT id,data FROM r WHERE t=?1 UNION ALL SELECT id,data FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 ORDER BY id" };
    let mut count = 0;
    let mut samples = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
    let mut statement = conn.prepare(query)?;
    for image in statement.query_map([t], |r| Ok((r.get::<_, String>(0)?, r.get::<_, String>(1)?)))? {
        let (id, data) = image?;
        let mut row: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        if let Some(table) = schema.as_ref().and_then(|s| s.table(table)) { crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, table); }
        for cell in conn.prepare("SELECT column_name,data FROM _dormant_values WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2")?.query_map(params![t, id], |r| Ok((r.get::<_, String>(0)?, r.get::<_, String>(1)?)))? {
            let (column, value) = cell?;
            if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut row {
                fields.entry(column).or_insert(serde_json::from_str(&value).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?);
            }
        }
        let entries = crate::rows::index_keys(&index, &row, &id).len() as u64;
        count += entries;
        if entries > 0 && samples.len() < 5 { samples.insert(super::adopt_refusals::bounded(&id)); }
    }
    Ok((count, samples.into_iter().collect()))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#91-per-row-narrowed-value-dormancy — only rows surviving every activation phase count as restored.
pub(crate) fn finalize_restorations(tx:&Tx<'_>) -> Result<(),RuntimeError> {
    let entries=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT key,value FROM _meta WHERE key LIKE 'activation_narrowed_restored_ids:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_column_restored_ids:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_table_restored_ids:%'")?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get::<_,String>(0)?,r.get::<_,String>(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
    let mut narrowed=0u64;
    for (key,ids) in entries {
        let (prefix,path)=key.split_once(':').expect("activation restoration key");
        let table=if prefix=="activation_column_restored_ids" {path.split_once('.').expect("column path").0} else {path};
        let count:u64=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(?2))",params![tx.num(table)?,ids],|r|r.get::<_,i64>(0).map(|n|n as u64))?;
        tx.set_meta(&format!("{}:{path}",prefix.trim_end_matches("_ids")),&count.to_string())?;
        if prefix=="activation_narrowed_restored_ids" {narrowed+=count;}
    }
    tx.set_meta("activation_narrowed_restored",&narrowed.to_string())?;
    Ok(())
}

pub(crate) fn event(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    operation: &str,
    release: Option<u64>,
    previous: Option<u64>,
    summary: serde_json::Value,
    items: &[DormantItem],
) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    let revision = tx
        .meta("release_revision")?
        .and_then(|v| v.parse::<u64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0)
        .checked_add(1)
        .ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Generation("release revision exhausted".into()))?;
    tx.set_meta("release_revision", &revision.to_string())?;
    let generation = tx
        .meta("generation")?
        .and_then(|v| v.parse::<u64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(1);
    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _release_events(at,operation,release,previous,generation,revision,summary) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5,?6,?7)",params![crate::now_ms(),operation,release.map(sql_number).transpose()?,previous.map(sql_number).transpose()?,sql_number(generation)?,sql_number(revision)?,summary.to_string()])?;
    let id = tx.conn().last_insert_rowid();
    for item in items {
        tx.conn().execute(
            "INSERT INTO _release_event_items VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",
            params![id, item.key, canonical_json(item)],
        )?;
    }
    Ok(revision)
}

impl Store {
    pub fn dormant_inventory(
        &mut self,
        after: Option<&str>,
        limit: usize,
    ) -> Result<Vec<DormantItem>, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.read_transaction()?;
        let items = inventory(tx.conn(), after, limit)?;
        tx.rollback()?;
        Ok(items)
    }
    /// One snapshot: current identity, bounded history and storage inventories.
    pub fn release_status(
        &mut self,
        after: u64,
        items_after: Option<&str>,
        event_id: Option<u64>,
    ) -> Result<serde_json::Value, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.read_transaction()?;
        let mut events = Vec::new();
        let mut inventory_page = Vec::<serde_json::Value>::new();
        if present(tx.conn())? {
            let mut s=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT id,at,operation,release,previous,generation,revision,summary FROM _release_events WHERE id>?1 ORDER BY id LIMIT 16")?;
            let mut rows = s.query([sql_number(after)?])?;
            while let Some(r) = rows.next()? {
                events.push(serde_json::json!({"id":r.get::<_,i64>(0)?,"at":r.get::<_,i64>(1)?,"operation":r.get::<_,String>(2)?,"release":r.get::<_,Option<i64>>(3)?,"previous":r.get::<_,Option<i64>>(4)?,"generation":r.get::<_,i64>(5)?,"revision":r.get::<_,i64>(6)?,"summary":serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&r.get::<_,String>(7)?).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?}));
            }
            if let Some(event) = event_id {
                let mut s=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT detail FROM _release_event_items WHERE event=?1 AND (?2 IS NULL OR path>?2) ORDER BY path LIMIT 16")?;
                for item in s.query_map(params![sql_number(event)?, items_after], |r| {
                    r.get::<_, String>(0)
                })? {
                    inventory_page.push(
                        serde_json::from_str(&item?)
                            .map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?,
                    );
                }
            } else {
                inventory_page = inventory(tx.conn(), items_after, 16)?
                    .into_iter()
                    .map(|i| serde_json::to_value(i).unwrap())
                    .collect();
            }
        }
        let workers:Vec<(String,String)>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT key,value FROM _meta WHERE key='worker:status' OR key LIKE 'worker:status:%' ORDER BY key LIMIT 16")?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
        let previous_active: Option<u64> = if present(tx.conn())? {tx.conn().query_row("SELECT previous FROM _release_events WHERE release IS NOT NULL AND previous != release AND operation != 'prune' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1",[],|r|r.get::<_,Option<i64>>(0)).optional()?.flatten().map(|n|n as u64)} else {None};
        let result = serde_json::json!({"previous_active":previous_active,"release":tx.meta("release")?.and_then(|v|v.parse::<u64>().ok()),"revision":tx.meta("release_revision")?.and_then(|v|v.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0),"generation":tx.meta("generation")?.and_then(|v|v.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(1),"native_digest":tx.meta("release_native_digest")?,"events_after":events.last().map(|e|e["id"].clone()),"events":events,"items_after":inventory_page.last().map(|i|i["key"].clone()),"items":inventory_page,"event":event_id,"workers":workers});
        tx.rollback()?;
        Ok(result)
    }
    pub fn prune_dormant(
        &mut self,
        confirm: bool,
        expected_revision: Option<u64>,
    ) -> Result<serde_json::Value, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.transaction()?;
        let held = tx
            .meta("release_revision")?
            .and_then(|v| v.parse::<u64>().ok())
            .unwrap_or(0);
        if expected_revision.is_some_and(|n| n != held) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation(
                "release revision changed; run releases and retry prune".into(),
            ));
        }
        let items = inventory(tx.conn(), None, usize::MAX)?;
        if !confirm {
            tx.rollback()?;
            return Ok(
                serde_json::json!({"pruned":false,"items":items,"rewrite":"snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive"}),
            );
        }
        if !present(tx.conn())? {
            tx.rollback()?;
            return Ok(serde_json::json!({"pruned":true,"items":[],"revision":held}));
        }
        // Release only dormant homes; active application data is untouched.
        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _assets SET released_at=COALESCE(released_at,?1) WHERE home_table IN (SELECT table_name FROM _dormant_tables) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values v JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=v.t WHERE c.name=_assets.home_table AND v.column_name=_assets.home_column AND v.id=_assets.home_id)",[crate::now_ms()])?;
        if super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())? {
            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _assets SET released_at=COALESCE(released_at,?1) WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=n.t WHERE c.name=_assets.home_table AND n.id=_assets.home_id)",[crate::now_ms()])?;
            tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_values WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=_dormant_values.t AND n.id=_dormant_values.id)",[])?;
            tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_narrow_rows",[])?;
        }
        let revision = event(
            &tx,
            "prune",
            tx.meta("release")?.and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()),
            None,
            serde_json::json!({"dropped_items":items.len(),"bytes":items.iter().map(|i|i.bytes).sum::<u64>(),"asset_bytes":items.iter().map(|i|i.asset_bytes).sum::<u64>()}),
            &items,
        )?;
        let event_id = tx.conn().last_insert_rowid();
        tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _dormant_values;DELETE FROM _dormant_rows;DELETE FROM _dormant_refs;DELETE FROM _dormant_columns;DELETE FROM _dormant_indexes;DELETE FROM _dormant_tables;")?;
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok(serde_json::json!({"pruned":true,"items":items,"revision":revision,"event":event_id}))
    }
}
                return Err(RuntimeError::Schema(
                    "scheduled work takes periodStart: time and periodEnd: time, or nothing".into(),
                ));
            }
        }
        let prior_items = std::cell::RefCell::new(Vec::new());
        let population=std::cell::Cell::new(None);
        let outcome=store.activate_at_with_launch(schema, Some(programs), false, now, allow_feed_adoption, |conn| {
            Store::check_selected_release(conn, expected_release)?;
            if let Some(backend) = expected_backend { backend.check(conn)?; }
            if let Some(options)=release {
                options.check(conn)?;
                *prior_items.borrow_mut()=crate::store::release_history::inventory(conn,None,usize::MAX)?;
                crate::store::dormant::enable(conn)?;
                conn.execute("INSERT INTO _meta(key,value) VALUES('release_native_digest',?1) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value=excluded.value",[options.native_digest.as_deref().unwrap_or("")])?;
                conn.execute("INSERT INTO _meta(key,value) VALUES('activation_restore',?1) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value=excluded.value",[if options.restore_dormant {"true"}else{"false"}])?;
            }
            Ok(())
        }, |tx, changed, report| {
            if let Some(options)=release {tx.set_meta("release",&options.release.to_string())?;}
            use rusqlite::OptionalExtension;
            let repair_live_sums = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'live_sum_candidates'", [], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)).optional()?.as_deref() != Some("1");
            if !changed && !repair_live_sums { return Ok(()); }
            let previous: Option<String> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation = (SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key = 'generation')", [], |row| row.get(0)).optional()?;
            let previous: Option<Schema> = previous.map(|text| serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::decode(e, RuntimeError::Store))).transpose()?;
            if changed {
                let epoch = tx.next_seq()?;
                for table in partition::changed_group_audiences(previous.as_ref(), schema) {
                    tx.set_meta(&partition::audience_epoch_key(&table), &epoch.to_string())?;
                    tx.set_meta(&partition::audience_epoch_time_key(&table), &now.to_string())?;
                }
            }
            let population_changed=tx.meta("activation_population")?.as_deref()==Some("true");
            let rebuild: Vec<_> = schema.maintains.iter().filter(|m| {
                (changed && (population_changed || previous.as_ref().is_none_or(|old| crate::successor::maintain_rebuild_needed(old, schema, m))))
                    || (repair_live_sums && matches!(m.kind, crate::schema::MaintainKind::Sum { .. }) && schema.table(&m.source).is_some_and(|source| source.is_expiring()))
            }).collect();
            tx.set_meta("live_sum_candidates", "1")?;
            let attribution = recorded::paths_changed(schema, previous.as_ref());
            let columns = column_activation::changed_parents(schema, if population_changed {None} else {previous.as_ref()});
            if rebuild.is_empty() && !attribution && columns.is_empty() { return Ok(()); }
            let seq = tx.next_seq()?;
            let phase = std::time::Instant::now();
            if attribution {
                // This runner owns only activation rewrites; ordinary rebuilds
                // below retain their commit and partition accounting.
                let mut capture = Runner::new(tx, schema, Context::system().at(now), Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(), Cut::Abort), seq);
                capture.capture_activation(previous.as_ref(), Some(report))?;
                report.detail("capture recorded paths", phase);
                capture.rebuild_recorded_activation(previous.as_ref(), &rebuild.iter().copied().filter(|m| m.private.is_none()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), report)?;
            }
            let mut runner = Runner::new(tx, schema, Context::system().at(now), Meter::new(Ceilings::unbounded(), Cut::Abort), seq);
            runner.activate_columns(previous.as_ref(), &rebuild, &columns, Some(report))?;
            for maintain in rebuild.into_iter().filter(|m| m.private.is_none()) {
                if !recorded::table_paths_changed(schema.table(&maintain.source), previous.as_ref().and_then(|s| s.table(&maintain.source))) {
                    runner.rebuild_maintain_reported(maintain, report)?;
                }
            }
            let events = runner.events.take();
            let tables: Vec<String> = runner.written_tables.iter().cloned().collect();
            let written = runner.rows_written;
            drop(runner);
            if written > 0 || !columns.is_empty() {
                tx.record_commit(seq, now, "rebuild", crate::SYSTEM_PRINCIPAL, "rebuild", &tables, written)?;
                partition::append(tx, seq, &events)?;
            }
            Ok(())
        }, |tx| {
            if let Some(options)=release {
                crate::store::release_history::finalize_restorations(tx)?;
                let mut items=crate::store::release_history::inventory(tx.conn(),None,usize::MAX)?;
                let dormant_items=items.len();
                let mut restored_items=0usize;
                for item in &mut items {
                    let before=prior_items.borrow().iter().find(|i|i.key==item.key).cloned();
                    let count=before.as_ref().map_or(0,|i|i.rows);
                    item.before_rows=Some(count);item.after_rows=Some(item.rows);
                    item.before_sample_ids=Some(before.map_or_else(Vec::new,|i|i.sample_ids));
                    let restored=if item.kind=="rows" {
                        tx.meta(&format!("activation_narrowed_restored:{}",item.path))?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)
                    } else if item.kind=="column" {tx.meta(&format!("activation_column_restored:{}",item.path))?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)} else {0};
                    item.restored_rows=Some(restored);
                    if restored>0 {item.action="partially restored".into();restored_items+=1;}
                }
                let remaining:std::collections::BTreeSet<_>=items.iter().map(|i|i.key.clone()).collect();
                for mut item in prior_items.borrow().iter().filter(|i|!remaining.contains(&i.key)).cloned() {
                    let restored=if matches!(item.kind.as_str(),"rows"|"column") {
                        let prefix=if item.kind=="rows" {"activation_narrowed_restored"} else {"activation_column_restored"};
                        tx.meta(&format!("{prefix}:{}",item.path))?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)
                    } else if item.kind=="table" {
                        tx.meta(&format!("activation_table_restored:{}",item.path))?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)
                    } else {item.rows};
                    item.action=if restored>0 || item.definition_only {"restored"} else {"removed without restoration"}.into();
                    item.before_rows=Some(item.rows);item.after_rows=Some(0);item.restored_rows=Some(restored);item.before_sample_ids=Some(item.sample_ids.clone());
                    if restored>0 || item.definition_only {restored_items+=1;}
                    items.push(item);
                }
                population.set(Some((tx.meta("activation_narrowed_retained")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0),tx.meta("activation_narrowed_restored")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0))));
                crate::store::release_history::event(tx,&options.operation,Some(options.release),options.expected_previous,serde_json::json!({"dormant_items":dormant_items,"restored_items":restored_items,"native_digest":options.native_digest,"narrowed_retained":tx.meta("activation_narrowed_retained")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0),"narrowed_restored":tx.meta("activation_narrowed_restored")?.and_then(|s|s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0)}),&items)?;
                tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key IN ('activation_restore','activation_population','activation_narrowed_retained','activation_narrowed_restored') OR key LIKE 'activation_narrowed_restored:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_column_restored:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_table_restored:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_table_restored_ids:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_narrowed_restored_ids:%' OR key LIKE 'activation_column_restored_ids:%'",[])?;
            }
            crate::store::opening::Metadata::read(tx)
        });
        if outcome.is_ok() {
            if let Some((retained,restored))=population.get().filter(|(a,b)|*a>0 || *b>0) {
                store.adoption_report.changes.insert(0,format!("{retained} newly dormant {}; {restored} narrowed {} restored", if retained == 1 { "row" } else { "rows" }, if restored == 1 { "row" } else { "rows" }));
            }
        }
        if outcome.is_ok() {
            if let Ok(items)=store.dormant_inventory(None,usize::MAX) {
                if release.is_some() { store.adoption_report.changes.insert(0, crate::store::adoption_report::AdoptionReport::dormant(&items)); }
            }
        }
        outcome
    }
}
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#10 — packed starters must pass their real web toolchains
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { createServer } from "node:net";
import { Worker } from "node:worker_threads";
import { loadavg } from "node:os";
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { setTimeout as sleep } from "node:timers/promises";
import { mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync, existsSync } from "node:fs";
import { resolve, join } from "node:path";

// The live keeper alone signals its own group. Closing its private pipe also
// cleans up if this runner dies; no cached PID ever authorizes a signal.
export async function runToolchainCommand(command, args, { cwd, env, timeout }) {
  const owner = fileURLToPath(new URL("./test-command-owner.mjs", import.meta.url));
  const keeper = spawn(process.execPath, [owner, "100", command, ...args], { cwd, env, detached: true, stdio: ["pipe", "inherit", "inherit", "ipc"] });
  let released = false, completed = false, failure, status;
  const release = mode => { if (!released) { released = true; keeper.stdin.end(mode); } };
  const fail = error => { failure ??= error; release("TERM"); };
  const timer = setTimeout(() => fail(Error(`${command}: toolchain command timed out`)), timeout);
  keeper.stdin.on("error", error => { failure ??= error; });
  keeper.on("message", message => {
    if (message.error) fail(Error(message.error));
    if (message.exited) { completed = true; status = message.status; release("KILL"); }
  });
  await new Promise(done => {
    keeper.once("error", error => { failure ??= error; });
    keeper.once("close", done);
  });
  clearTimeout(timer);
  if (!released || !completed) failure ??= Error(`${command}: command owner exited without a result`);
  // This read-only group-existence probe needs no /proc mount in the clean
  // userspace. Refuse on lingering members or ambiguous/permission errors.
  // Two empty observations also protect against a transient inspection result.
  const deadline = performance.now() + 5_000;
  let empty = 0;
  if (keeper.pid) while (empty < 2) {
    try { process.kill(-keeper.pid, 0); empty = 0; }
    catch (error) { if (error.code === "ESRCH") empty++; else { failure ??= error; break; } }
    if (empty === 2) break;
    if (performance.now() >= deadline) { failure ??= Error(`${command}: toolchain process group did not drain`); break; }
    await sleep(50);
  }
  if (failure) throw failure;
  if (status !== 0) throw Error(`${command}: toolchain command exited ${status}`);
}

// Every supported frontend/template pair; schema-only init has no frontend proof.
export const starterCases = [
  { id: "web", kind: "web", flags: ["--web"] },
  { id: "web-online", kind: "web", flags: ["--web", "--online"] },
  { id: "expo", kind: "expo", flags: ["--expo"] },
  { id: "web-chat", kind: "web", template: "chat", flags: ["--web", "--template", "chat"] },
  { id: "expo-chat", kind: "expo", template: "chat", flags: ["--expo", "--template", "chat"] },
  { id: "expo-social", kind: "expo", template: "social", flags: ["--template", "social"] },
];
async function boundedStop(work, label) {
  let timer;
  try { return await Promise.race([work, new Promise((_, reject) => { timer = setTimeout(() => reject(Error(label + " timed out")), 10_000); })]); }
  finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
}
export async function proveFrontendTwice(run, cwd, starter) {
  const results = [];
  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 2; attempt++) {
    const occupied = createServer();
    const low = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => { occupied.once("error", reject); occupied.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", () => resolve(occupied.address().port)); });
    const high = Math.min(65535, low + 20);
    const workers = Array.from({ length: 2 }, () => new Worker(`setInterval(() => { const until = performance.now() + 25; while (performance.now() < until) {} }, 50);`, { eval: true }));
    const started = Date.now(), load = loadavg();
    try {
      await run("npm", ["run", "frontend:proof"], cwd, { PORT_LO: String(low), PORT_HI: String(high) });
      results.push({ starter: starter.id, kind: starter.kind, attempt, status: "passed", elapsedMs: Date.now() - started, range: [low, high], occupied: low, cpuLoadWorkers: workers.length, loadavg: load, logs: join(cwd, "logs") });
    } finally {
      const stopped = await Promise.allSettled([...workers.map(worker => boundedStop(worker.terminate(), "CPU worker stop")), boundedStop(new Promise(resolve => occupied.close(resolve)), "occupied listener close")]);
      const failures = stopped.filter(result => result.status === "rejected").map(result => result.reason);
      if (failures.length) throw new AggregateError(failures, "loaded-host proof cleanup failed");
    }
  }
  return results;
}

// Runs outside a checkout, using only packed release artifacts. Registry access
// is needed for Expo/Vite dependencies; npm prefers cached downloads when present.
export async function proveStarters(client, platform, expo, directory, browserProofs = true) {
  const env = { ...process.env, CI: "1", EXPO_NO_TELEMETRY: "1", EXPO_OFFLINE: "1", npm_config_audit: "false", npm_config_fund: "false" };
  delete env.SNAPBACK4_BIN;
  const deadline = performance.now() + 1_200_000;
  const run = (cmd, args, cwd, overrides = {}) => {
    const remaining = Math.ceil(deadline - performance.now());
    if (remaining <= 0) throw Error("starter qualification exceeded its twenty-minute guard");
    return runToolchainCommand(cmd, args, { cwd, env: { ...env, ...overrides }, timeout: Math.min(300_000, remaining) });
  };
  const artifacts = [client, platform, expo].map(path => resolve(path));
  const results = [];
  for (const starter of starterCases) {
    const { kind, template, id, flags } = starter;
    const cwd = join(directory, `starter-${id}`); mkdirSync(cwd);
    writeFileSync(join(cwd, "package.json"), '{"private":true}');
    // Install the CLI first so init and all later package scripts resolve the
    // packed binary. Pin native JS in init's manifest before dependency install.
    await run("npm", ["install", "--no-save", "--ignore-scripts", "--no-audit", "--no-fund", ...artifacts.slice(0, 2)], cwd);
    const cli = join(cwd, "node_modules/.bin/snapback4");
    await run(cli, ["init", ...flags], cwd);
    const pkg = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(cwd, "package.json")));
    pkg.dependencies.snapback4 = `file:${artifacts[0]}`;
    pkg.optionalDependencies = { [`snapback4-${process.platform}-${process.arch}`]: `file:${artifacts[1]}` };
    if (kind === "expo") pkg.dependencies["snapback4-expo"] = `file:${artifacts[2]}`;
    writeFileSync(join(cwd, "package.json"), JSON.stringify(pkg, null, 2));
    // Lifecycle scripts are enabled: esbuild and the other real tools need them.
    await run("npm", ["install", "--prefer-offline", "--no-audit", "--no-fund"], cwd);
    const build = async () => {
      await run(cli, ["check"], cwd);
      await run("npm", ["run", "typecheck"], cwd);
      if (kind === "expo") await run(join(cwd, "node_modules/.bin/expo"), ["export", "-p", "web", "--max-workers", "2"], cwd);
      await run("npm", ["run", kind === "expo" ? "build:web" : "build"], cwd);

    await build();
    const adaptedProofs = browserProofs ? await proveFrontendTwice(run, cwd, starter) : [];
    results.push({ starter: id, kind, status: "passed", adapted: "passed", typecheck: "passed", build: "passed", frontendProofs, adaptedProofs });
  }
  return results;
}

import { mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";

import { runToolchainCommand, starterCases, proveFrontendTwice } from "./publication-starters.mjs";
export async function proveGeneratedProofs(client, platform, expo, directory) {
  const artifacts = [client, platform, expo].map(path => resolve(path));
  const env = { ...process.env, CI: "1", EXPO_NO_TELEMETRY: "1", EXPO_OFFLINE: "1", npm_config_audit: "false", npm_config_fund: "false" };

    const cwd = join(directory, `frontend-${id}`); mkdirSync(cwd, { recursive: true });
    writeFileSync(join(cwd, "package.json"), '{"private":true}');
    await run("npm", ["install", "--no-save", "--ignore-scripts", "--no-audit", "--no-fund", ...artifacts.slice(0, 2)], cwd);
    const cli = join(cwd, "node_modules/.bin/snapback4");

    assert.equal(pkg.devDependencies["playwright-core"], "1.55.1");
    assert.ok(!readFileSync(join(cwd, "scripts/frontend-proof.mjs"), "utf8").includes("29040"));
    pkg.dependencies.snapback4 = `file:${artifacts[0]}`;
    pkg.optionalDependencies = { [`snapback4-${process.platform}-${process.arch}`]: `file:${artifacts[1]}` };
    if (kind === "expo") pkg.dependencies["snapback4-expo"] = `file:${artifacts[2]}`;

    await run(cli, ["check"], cwd);
    await run("npm", ["run", "typecheck"], cwd);
    await run("npm", ["run", kind === "web" ? "build" : "build:web"], cwd);
    results.push(...await proveFrontendTwice(run, cwd, starter));
  }
  return results;
}
if (process.argv[1] && resolve(process.argv[1]) === new URL(import.meta.url).pathname) {
  const [client, platform, expo, out] = process.argv.slice(2);
  if (!client || !platform || !expo || !out) throw Error("usage: publication-frontend-proofs.mjs client.tgz platform.tgz expo.tgz output-directory");

  writeFileSync(join(resolve(out), "frontend-proofs.json"), JSON.stringify(results, null, 2));
  console.log(JSON.stringify(results));
}

// @ref LLP 3000.021#3.3 — only Darwin is executed and signature-checked by the publisher
export async function proveDarwin(staged, work) {

const consumer = join(work, "consumer");
mkdirSync(consumer);
writeFileSync(join(consumer, "package.json"), JSON.stringify({ name: "consumer", private: true, type: "module" }));
const env = { ...process.env }; delete env.SNAPBACK4_BIN;
run("npm", ["install", "--no-audit", "--no-fund", "--loglevel=error", platform.tarball, staged.client.tarball, "react@19.3.0", "react-dom@19.3.0", "@types/react@19.3.0", "typescript@5.9.3"], { cwd: consumer, env });
const installedBinary = join(consumer, "node_modules/snapback4-darwin-arm64/snapback4");
if (digest(installedBinary) !== platform.binarySha256) throw new Error("packed/installed platform binary differs from the production artifact");

if (signature.signingAuthority !== platform.signing.signingAuthority || signature.signingTeamId !== platform.signing.signingTeamId) throw new Error("installed binary signature differs from the staged signature");
const installedVersion = run(join(consumer, "node_modules/.bin/snapback4"), ["--version"], { cwd: consumer, env }).trim();
if (installedVersion !== `snapback4 ${staged.version}`) throw new Error(`installed CLI is ${installedVersion}`);
if (statSync(installedBinary).size !== platform.binaryBytes) throw new Error("installed binary size differs from qualified size");
writeFileSync(join(consumer, "tsconfig.json"), JSON.stringify({ compilerOptions: { target: "ES2022", module: "ESNext", moduleResolution: "Bundler", strict: true, jsx: "react-jsx", noEmit: true, skipLibCheck: true, types: ["react"] }, include: ["check.tsx"] }));
writeFileSync(join(consumer, "check.tsx"), `
import { isReady, type Read, type Write, type Query } from "snapback4/contract";
import { createMockClient, page } from "snapback4/mock";
import { SnapbackProvider, useQuery, useMutation, useLink } from "snapback4/react";
const q: Query<{ c?: null }, string[]> = { kind: "query", name: "t.list" };
const client = createMockClient({ viewer: null, tables: {}, ops: { queries: { "t.list": () => page(["a"], null) }, mutations: {} } });
export function Screen() { const r = useQuery(q, {}); const link = useLink(); const m = useMutation({ kind: "mutation", name: "t.add" }); return <SnapbackProvider client={client}>{isReady(r) ? r.data.join() : link}{String(m.inFlight)}</SnapbackProvider>; }
const w: Write = { state: "pending", id: "x" }; const rd: Read<number> = { loading: true }; void w; void rd;
`);
run(join(consumer, "node_modules/.bin/tsc"), ["-p", "tsconfig.json"], { cwd: consumer });
writeFileSync(join(consumer, "run.mjs"), `
import { createMockClient, page } from "snapback4/mock";
import { isReady } from "snapback4/contract";
const c = createMockClient({ viewer: "p1", tables: {}, ops: { queries: { "t.list": () => page([1, 2, 3], null) }, mutations: { "t.add": (t, a, v, mint) => { const id = mint(); (t.t ??= new Map()).set(id, { id, n: a.n }); return [id]; } } }, commitDelayMs: 1 });
const r = await c.query({ kind: "query", name: "t.list" }, {});
if (!isReady(r) || r.data.length !== 3 || !r.fresh) throw new Error("mock query broken: " + JSON.stringify(r));
const w = await c.mutate({ kind: "mutation", name: "t.add" }, { n: 1 });
if (w.state !== "sent") throw new Error("mock mutate broken: " + JSON.stringify(w));
c.setLink("offline");
const w2 = await c.mutate({ kind: "mutation", name: "t.add" }, { n: 2 });
if (w2.state !== "pending") throw new Error("offline admission broken: " + JSON.stringify(w2));
console.log("consumer ok: query complete+fresh, online write sent, offline write pending");
`);
console.log(run("node", ["run.mjs"], { cwd: consumer }).trim());

copyFileSync(new URL("./publication-roundtrip.mjs", import.meta.url), join(consumer, "roundtrip.mjs"));
run("node", ["roundtrip.mjs", staged.version], { cwd: consumer, env, timeout: 90_000 });
const starters = await proveStarters(staged.client.tarball, platform.tarball, staged.expo.tarball, work);
return { status: "passed", starters, expoIntegrity: staged.expo.integrity, clientIntegrity: staged.client.integrity, platformIntegrity: platform.integrity };
}

import { join, resolve, dirname } from "node:path";

export function containerRuntime(probeRuntime = name => spawnSync(name, ["info"], { stdio: "ignore", timeout: 15_000 })) {
  for (const name of ["docker", "podman"]) {
    const probe = probeRuntime(name);
    if (probe.status === 0) return name;
  }
  return "proot";
}
export function containerProof(runtime, client, platform, version, work, expo) {
  const context = join(work, "container"); mkdirSync(context);
  for (const file of ["test-command-owner.mjs", "test-owned-command.mjs", "test-command-group.mjs"])
    copyFileSync(new URL(`./${file}`, import.meta.url), join(context, file));
  copyFileSync(expo, join(context, "expo.tgz"));
  copyFileSync(new URL("./publication-starters.mjs", import.meta.url), join(context, "starters.mjs"));
  copyFileSync(client, join(context, "client.tgz")); copyFileSync(platform, join(context, "platform.tgz"));
  copyFileSync(new URL("./publication-roundtrip.mjs", import.meta.url), join(context, "roundtrip.mjs"));
  const proofs = [];
  for (const base of ["ubuntu:22.04", "alpine"]) {
    const started = Date.now();
    const dockerfile = base === "alpine"
      ? `FROM node:${nodeVersion}-alpine AS node\nFROM alpine:3.23\nCOPY --from=node /usr/local /usr/local\nRUN apk add --no-cache libstdc++ ca-certificates\n`
      : `FROM node:${nodeVersion}-bookworm-slim AS node\nFROM ubuntu:22.04\nCOPY --from=node /usr/local /usr/local\nRUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends libstdc++6 ca-certificates && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*\n`;
    writeFileSync(join(context, "Dockerfile"), dockerfile + `COPY client.tgz platform.tgz expo.tgz roundtrip.mjs starters.mjs test-command-owner.mjs test-owned-command.mjs test-command-group.mjs /proof/\nWORKDIR /consumer\nENV NPM_CONFIG_USERCONFIG=/dev/null NPM_CONFIG_GLOBALCONFIG=/tmp/snapback4-empty-global.npmrc\n`);
    const tag = `snapback4-proof-${process.pid}-${base.startsWith("ubuntu") ? "ubuntu" : "alpine"}`;
    try {
      run(runtime, ["build", "--pull", "--platform", "linux/amd64", "-t", tag, context], { stdio: "inherit" });
      const image = run(runtime, ["image", "inspect", "--format", "{{.Id}}", tag]).trim();
      // The only host files in the image are these proof inputs. No checkout,
      // home, npmrc, binary override or host credentials enter the container.
      run(runtime, ["run", "--rm", "--name", tag, "--platform", "linux/amd64", tag, "sh", "-ec",
        `test -z "\${SNAPBACK4_BIN+x}"; test "$(node -v)" = v${nodeVersion}; printf '{"private":true,"type":"module"}' > package.json; npm install --ignore-scripts --no-audit --no-fund /proof/client.tgz /proof/platform.tgz; cp /proof/roundtrip.mjs .; node roundtrip.mjs ${version}; node /proof/starters.mjs /proof/client.tgz /proof/platform.tgz /proof/expo.tgz --build-only`], { stdio: "inherit", timeout: 1_200_000 });
      proofs.push({ base, image, runtime, route: runtime, elapsedMs: Date.now() - started, node: nodeVersion, status: "passed", clientIntegrity: sri(client), platformIntegrity: sri(platform), expoIntegrity: sri(expo), starters: { web: "passed", expo: "passed", adapted: "passed", templates: "passed" } });
    } finally {
      spawnSync(runtime, ["rm", "--force", tag], { stdio: "ignore", timeout: 30_000 });
      spawnSync(runtime, ["image", "rm", tag], { stdio: "ignore", timeout: 30_000 });
    }
  }
  return proofs;
}
export async function qualify(args = process.argv.slice(2)) {
  refuseCredentials(); // Must precede all commands, including argument-dependent work.

  const consumerOnly = args.includes("--consumer-only");
  args = args.filter(arg => arg !== "--consumer-only");
  const options = {};
  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 2) {
    if (!["--client", "--expo", "--out"].includes(args[i]) || !args[i + 1] || args[i + 1].startsWith("--")) throw new Error("usage: qualify-linux.mjs --client /path/client.tgz --expo /path/expo.tgz --out /path/new-directory [--consumer-only]");
    options[args[i]] = resolve(args[i + 1]);
  }
  if (!options["--client"] || !options["--expo"] || !options["--out"]) throw new Error("usage: qualify-linux.mjs --client /path/client.tgz --expo /path/expo.tgz --out /path/new-directory [--consumer-only]");
  const root = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), ".."), out = options["--out"], client = options["--client"], expo = options["--expo"];
  if (existsSync(join(out, "receipt.json"))) throw new Error("qualification receipt already exists; choose a new --out directory");
  const source = cleanSource(root), version = json(join(root, "packages/snapback4/package.json")).version;
  if (!/^\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:-[A-Za-z0-9.-]+)?$/.test(version)) throw new Error("invalid release version");
  clientManifest(client, version);

  const clientIntegrity = sri(client), expoIntegrity = sri(expo), runtime = containerRuntime();
  const env = { ...process.env, LIBSQLITE3_FLAGS: sqliteBuildFlags }; delete env.SNAPBACK4_BIN;
  mkdirSync(out, { recursive: true });
  const log = join(out, "suite.log");
  let result = { status: null }, summary;
  if (!consumerOnly) {
    const fd = openSync(log, "w");
    try { result = spawnSync("bash", [join(root, "scripts/test.sh")], { cwd: root, env, stdio: ["ignore", fd, fd] }); }
    finally { closeSync(fd); }
    summary = readFileSync(log, "utf8").match(/^== \d+ failure\(s\) in \d+ s$/m)?.[0];
  }
  const suitePassed = result.status === 0 && summary?.startsWith("== 0 ");
  run("bash", [join(root, "scripts/build-linux-musl.sh")], { cwd: root, env, stdio: "inherit" });
  const binary = join(root, `target/${linuxTarget}/production/snapback4`), binarySha256 = digest(binary), elf = checkElf(readFileSync(binary));
  if (run(binary, ["--version"]).trim() !== `snapback4 ${version}`) throw new Error("Linux built binary version differs from client");
  const work = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "snapback4-linux-"));
  try {
    const pkg = join(work, platforms[1]); mkdirSync(pkg);
    for (const name of ["package.json", "THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES"]) copyFileSync(join(root, "packages", platforms[1], name), join(pkg, name));
    copyFileSync(binary, join(pkg, "snapback4"));
    const [packed] = JSON.parse(run("npm", ["pack", "--ignore-scripts", "--json", "--pack-destination", work], { cwd: pkg }));
    const tarball = join(work, packed.filename), platform = artifact(tarball, platforms[1], version);
    // The container starter-toolchain proof remains beside this loaded-host
    // browser proof; both execute the same retained client/platform bytes.
    const frontendProofs = await proveGeneratedProofs(client, tarball, expo, out);
    save(join(out, "frontend-proofs.json"), frontendProofs);
    // Browser execution stays on the Linux host: clean userspaces qualify the
    // distribution toolchains without importing a host browser or its libraries.
    const starterProofs = await proveStarters(client, tarball, expo, out);
    save(join(out, "starter-proofs.json"), starterProofs);
    validateStarterProofs(starterProofs);
    const proofs = runtime === "proot" ? prootProof(client, tarball, version, undefined, undefined, expo) : containerProof(runtime, client, tarball, version, work, expo);
    // Keep executed consumer evidence on a red suite, without a success receipt.
    // An isolated flake rerun cannot turn the original suite into a release pass.
    save(join(out, "proofs.json"), { source, route: runtime, proofs, suite: { exitCode: result.status, summary } });
    // Integration may commission only installed-consumer proofs. This path
    // cannot produce the receipt publication imports as suite qualification.
    if (consumerOnly) {
      validateFrontendProofs(frontendProofs);
      if (cleanSource(root) !== source || sri(client) !== clientIntegrity || sri(expo) !== expoIntegrity)
        throw new Error("release source or client changed during consumer qualification");
      copyFileSync(tarball, join(out, packed.filename));
      const evidence = { format: 1, mode: "consumer-only", source, version, route: runtime,
        clientIntegrity, expoIntegrity, platform, binarySha256, elf, frontendProofs, starterProofs, proofs,
        suite: { status: "not-run" }, publicationQualified: false };
      save(join(out, "consumer.json"), evidence);
      console.log(JSON.stringify(evidence, null, 2));
      return evidence;
    }
    if (!suitePassed) throw new Error(`Linux source suite failed: ${summary ?? result.error?.message ?? result.status}; see ${log}; consumer evidence in proofs.json (no qualification receipt)`);
    const cc = process.env.SNAPBACK4_MUSL_CC ?? join(process.env.HOME, ".local/share/snapback4-musl-1.2.5/bin/musl-gcc");
    const receipt = { format: 1, route: runtime, source, target: linuxTarget, toolchain: { rustc: run("rustc", ["-Vv"]).trim(), cargo: run("cargo", ["-V"]).trim(), cc: run(cc, ["--version"]).trim(), linker: cc }, flags: { LIBSQLITE3_FLAGS: sqliteBuildFlags, RUSTFLAGS: linuxRustFlags }, suite: { status: "passed", exitCode: result.status, summary }, proofs, frontendProofs, starterProofs, elf, platform, binarySha256, clientIntegrity, expo: artifact(expo, "snapback4-expo", version) };
    if (cleanSource(root) !== source || sri(client) !== clientIntegrity || sri(expo) !== expoIntegrity) throw new Error("release source or client changed during Linux qualification");
    validateLinux(receipt, tarball, { version, integrity: clientIntegrity }, source);
    copyFileSync(tarball, join(out, packed.filename));

if (process.argv[1] && realpathSync(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) await qualify();
// @ref LLP 3000.021#3.3 — one client digest, platform-local qualification, digest-only import
import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { readFileSync, statSync, writeFileSync, renameSync } from "node:fs";
import { basename, join } from "node:path";
import { matchesPinnedProof } from "./linux-proof-inputs.mjs";
import { starterCases } from "./publication-starters.mjs";
import { checkElf } from "./check-elf.mjs";
export const sqliteBuildFlags = "-USQLITE_ENABLE_MEMORY_MANAGEMENT";
export const platforms = ["snapback4-darwin-arm64", "snapback4-linux-x64"];
export const linuxTarget = "x86_64-unknown-linux-musl";
export const linuxRustFlags = "-C target-feature=+crt-static -C relocation-model=static";
export const nodeVersion = "24.13.1";
export const run = (cmd, args, opts = {}) => execFileSync(cmd, args, { encoding: "utf8", stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "inherit"], ...opts });
export const hash = (bytes) => createHash("sha256").update(bytes).digest("hex");
export const digest = (path) => hash(readFileSync(path));
export const sri = (path) => `sha512-${createHash("sha512").update(readFileSync(path)).digest("base64")}`;
export const json = (path) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(path, "utf8"));
export function save(path, value) {
  const temporary = `${path}.tmp-${process.pid}`;
  writeFileSync(temporary, JSON.stringify(value, null, 2) + "\n");
  renameSync(temporary, path);
}
export function cleanSource(root) {
  if (run("git", ["status", "--porcelain", "--untracked-files=normal", "--", "."], { cwd: root }).trim())
    throw new Error("publication source is dirty; commit the release changes before preparing or qualifying artifacts");
  return run("git", ["rev-parse", "HEAD"], { cwd: root }).trim();
}
export function member(tarball, path) {
  return execFileSync("tar", ["-xOf", tarball, `package/${path}`], { maxBuffer: 128 * 1024 * 1024 });
}
export function manifest(tarball, name, version) {
  const m = JSON.parse(member(tarball, "package.json"));
  if (m.name !== name || m.version !== version) throw new Error(`packed manifest must name ${name}@${version}`);
  return m;
}
export function clientManifest(tarball, version, expected = platforms) {
  const m = manifest(tarball, "snapback4", version);
  const deps = m.optionalDependencies ?? {};
  if (JSON.stringify(Object.keys(deps).sort()) !== JSON.stringify([...expected].sort()) || expected.some(name => deps[name] !== version))
    throw new Error(`packed client optionalDependencies must be exactly ${expected.join(", ")} at ${version}`);
  return m;
}
export function artifact(tarball, name, version, extra = {}) {
  return { name, version, filename: basename(tarball), integrity: sri(tarball), bytes: statSync(tarball).size, ...extra };
}
export function verifyArtifact(a, directory, version) {
  if (!a || a.version !== version || typeof a.filename !== "string" || basename(a.filename) !== a.filename || !/^sha512-[A-Za-z0-9+/]{86}==$/.test(a.integrity ?? ""))
    throw new Error("publication resume has an incomplete artifact");
  const tarball = join(directory, a.filename);
  if (sri(tarball) !== a.integrity || statSync(tarball).size !== a.bytes)
    throw new Error(`staged ${a.name}@${version} tarball does not match its durable integrity`);
  return { ...a, tarball };
}
export function refuseCredentials(env = process.env) {
  for (const key of ["NPM_TOKEN", "NODE_AUTH_TOKEN"]) if (Object.hasOwn(env, key))
    throw new Error(`Linux qualification refuses ${key}; unset it before qualification (no authenticated registry writes)`);
}
function validAttempts(proofs, starter) {
  return Array.isArray(proofs) && proofs.length === 2 && [1, 2].every(attempt => proofs.some(p =>
    p.starter === starter.id && p.kind === starter.kind && p.attempt === attempt && p.status === "passed" && Array.isArray(p.range) && p.range.length === 2
    && Number.isInteger(p.range[0]) && Number.isInteger(p.range[1]) && p.range[0] > 0 && p.range[1] <= 65535 && p.range[0] < p.range[1]
    && p.occupied === p.range[0] && p.cpuLoadWorkers >= 1));
}
export function validateFrontendProofs(proofs) {
  if (!Array.isArray(proofs) || proofs.length !== starterCases.length * 2 || !starterCases.every(s => validAttempts(proofs.filter(p => p.starter === s.id), s)))
    throw new Error("Linux receipt needs every generated frontend/template proof twice under occupied-port and CPU load");
}
export function validateStarterProofs(starters) {
  if (!Array.isArray(starters) || starters.length !== starterCases.length) throw Error("Linux receipt needs every starter toolchain and frontend proof");
  validateFrontendProofs(starters.flatMap(s => s.frontendProofs ?? []));
  for (const starter of starterCases) {
    const result = starters.find(s => s.starter === starter.id);
    if (!result || result.status !== "passed" || result.typecheck !== "passed" || result.build !== "passed"
        || (["web", "expo"].includes(starter.id) && !validAttempts(result.adaptedProofs, starter)))
      throw Error("Linux receipt needs every starter toolchain and frontend proof, including adapted Home");
  }
}
export function validateLinux(receipt, tarball, client, source) {
  if (receipt.format !== 1 || receipt.platform?.name !== platforms[1] || receipt.platform.version !== client.version || receipt.source !== source || !/^[a-f0-9]{40}$/.test(source ?? ""))
    throw new Error("Linux receipt source/version/platform does not match the prepared release");
  if (receipt.target !== linuxTarget || receipt.flags?.LIBSQLITE3_FLAGS !== sqliteBuildFlags || receipt.flags?.RUSTFLAGS !== linuxRustFlags || !receipt.toolchain?.rustc || !receipt.toolchain?.cc || !receipt.toolchain?.linker)
    throw new Error("Linux receipt target/toolchain/SQLite flags are incomplete or differ");
  if (receipt.suite?.status !== "passed" || receipt.suite.exitCode !== 0 || !/^== 0 failure\(s\) in \d+ s$/.test(receipt.suite.summary ?? ""))
    throw new Error("Linux receipt does not carry a successful suite result");
  if (receipt.clientIntegrity !== client.integrity) throw new Error("Linux receipt proved a different client SRI");
  if (sri(tarball) !== receipt.platform.integrity) throw new Error("Linux tarball does not match receipt SRI");
  if (receipt.expo?.name !== "snapback4-expo" || receipt.expo.version !== client.version || !/^sha512-[A-Za-z0-9+/]{86}==$/.test(receipt.expo.integrity ?? "")) throw new Error("Linux receipt needs the packed Expo proof artifact");
  if (client.expoIntegrity && receipt.expo.integrity !== client.expoIntegrity) throw new Error("Linux receipt proved a different Expo SRI");
  // Older staged publications predate this qualification; new releases cannot
  // import a receipt that silently omits the loaded-host browser proof.
  const [major, minor, patch] = client.version.split("-")[0].split(".").map(Number);
  if (major > 0 || minor > 4 || minor === 4 && patch >= 28) { validateFrontendProofs(receipt.frontendProofs); validateStarterProofs(receipt.starterProofs); }
  const proofs = receipt.proofs;
  const proot = receipt.route === "proot";
  const bases = proot ? ["ubuntu:24.04", "alpine"] : ["ubuntu:22.04", "alpine"];
  if (!Array.isArray(proofs) || proofs.length !== 2 || !bases.every(base => proofs.some(p =>
    p.base === base && p.status === "passed" && p.clientIntegrity === client.integrity && p.platformIntegrity === receipt.platform.integrity
    && p.expoIntegrity === receipt.expo.integrity && p.starters?.web === "passed" && p.starters?.expo === "passed" && p.starters?.adapted === "passed" && p.starters?.templates === "passed"
    && (proot ? p.runtime === "proot" && p.route === "proot" && matchesPinnedProof(p)
      : p.node === nodeVersion && /^(docker|podman)$/.test(p.runtime) && p.image && (!receipt.route || receipt.route === p.runtime)))))
    throw new Error(`Linux receipt needs successful Ubuntu ${proot ? "24.04" : "22.04"} and Alpine proofs bound to both artifacts and pinned Node/runtimes/root filesystems`);
  if (receipt.elf?.status !== "passed" || receipt.elf.machine !== "x86_64" || receipt.elf.interpreter !== null || !Array.isArray(receipt.elf.needed) || receipt.elf.needed.length)
    throw new Error("Linux receipt must record ELF with no interpreter and no NEEDED entries");
  const m = manifest(tarball, platforms[1], client.version);
  if (JSON.stringify(m.os) !== '["linux"]' || JSON.stringify(m.cpu) !== '["x64"]') throw new Error("Linux packed manifest must select linux/x64");
  const binary = member(tarball, "snapback4");
  if (!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(receipt.binarySha256 ?? "") || hash(binary) !== receipt.binarySha256)
    throw new Error("Linux packed binary does not match receipt sha256");
  // Pure byte inspection also runs on the Mac; the receipt is not ELF evidence.
  const elf = checkElf(binary);
  if (elf.interpreter !== null || elf.needed.length) throw new Error("Linux packed ELF must have no interpreter or NEEDED entries");
  return receipt;
}
