//! Starter leftovers on the init-first path: `init --web` (or `--expo`) wrote
//! the todo starter, then the project replaced `snapback/schema.q`. The
//! starter's own files that still match the bytes init wrote, but no longer
//! fit the schema, are leftovers: `check` names them and `init --adapt`
//! removes or rewrites them. A file the project changed is never touched.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.2 — leftovers are judged by the starter's original bytes, never by name alone.
use super::starter::{self, Body, Platform};
use super::{OwnSchema, own_schema};
use std::path::Path;

/// One unmodified starter file that no longer fits the project's schema.
pub(crate) struct Leftover {
    pub path: &'static str,
    pub why: String,
    /// The text `init --adapt` writes; `None` removes the file.
    pub rewrite: Option<String>,
}

/// Starter operations its fixtures, cases and `Todos` screen call.
const STARTER_OPERATIONS: [&str; 4] = ["list", "add", "toggle", "remove"];

/// Whether a schema still has the starter's `todos` table and the operations
/// its fixtures, cases and `Todos` screen call.
pub(super) fn fits_todos(own: &OwnSchema) -> bool {
    own.tables.iter().any(|table| table == "todos")
        && STARTER_OPERATIONS.iter().all(|op| own.operations.iter().any(|name| name == op))
}

/// What the schema in `dir` leaves of the starter, and the advice that goes
/// with it (a sign-in the project must write by hand). No schema: nothing.
pub(crate) fn leftovers(dir: &Path) -> (Vec<Leftover>, Vec<String>) {
    let (found, advice, _) = plan(dir);
    (found, advice)
}

fn plan(dir: &Path) -> (Vec<Leftover>, Vec<String>, Vec<String>) {
    let Ok(Some(own)) = own_schema(dir) else { return (Vec::new(), Vec::new(), Vec::new()) };
    let mut found = Vec::new();
    let mut advice = Vec::new();
    let mut modified = Vec::new();
    let todos = fits_todos(&own);
    for (path, text) in starter::FIXTURES {
        let current = read(dir, path);
        if current.as_ref().is_some_and(|current| *current != super::without_design_references(text)) { modified.push(path.to_owned()); }
        if !todos && current.is_some_and(|current| current == super::without_design_references(text)) {
            let what = if path.ends_with("todos.jsonl") { "rows for the todo starter's `todos` table" } else { "cases for the todo starter's list/add/toggle/remove" };
            found.push(Leftover { path, why: format!("the unmodified {what}; your snapback/*.q has no todos table with those operations, so `snapback4 test` would refuse it"), rewrite: None });
        }
    }
    for platform in [Platform::Web, Platform::Expo] {
        if !screens(dir, platform, &own, todos, &mut found, &mut advice) {
            for path in [platform.paths().0, platform.paths().1] { if read(dir, path).is_some() { modified.push(path.to_owned()); } }
        }
    }
    if let Some(text) = read(dir, "src/native-smoke.ts") {
        let wanted = starter::native_smoke(own.identity.is_some_and(|(password, guests)| password && !guests));
        let recognized = [false, true].into_iter().any(|password| text == starter::native_smoke(password));
        if !recognized { modified.push("src/native-smoke.ts".to_owned()); }
        if text != wanted && recognized {
            found.push(Leftover { path: "src/native-smoke.ts", why: "the unmodified native smoke helper uses the previous schema's sign-in".into(), rewrite: Some(wanted) });
        }
    }
    readme(dir, &own, todos, &mut found, &mut modified);
    app_json(dir, &mut found);
    expo_favicon(dir, &mut found);
    (found, advice, modified)
}

/// The starter README, while it is still one init wrote (for either screen,
/// at the ports it names), when it describes another screen than the schema
/// now gets: the Todos README beside a project's schema, or the reverse.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — wave 6 (042): adapt rewrote the screen but left a README about todos and guest sign-in.
fn readme(dir: &Path, own: &OwnSchema, todos: bool, found: &mut Vec<Leftover>, modified: &mut Vec<String>) {
    let platform = if dir.join("src/App.tsx").is_file() { Platform::Web } else if dir.join("App.tsx").is_file() { Platform::Expo } else { return };
    let title = super::title(dir);
    let personas = own.identity.is_none() && !own.personas.is_empty();
    let wanted_body = if todos { Body::Todos } else { Body::Home(None) };
    for path in ["README.md", "README.snapback.md"] {
        let Some(text) = read(dir, path) else { continue };
        let port = number_after(&text, "127.0.0.1:");
        let web_port = number_after(&text, "localhost:");
        let ports = [(port, web_port), (None, web_port), (port, None), (None, None)];
        // The offline paragraph names the schema's synced tables when init
        // wrote it; any of them (or 0.4.22's unconditional claim) still matches.
        let masked = starter::without_offline(&text);
        let explicit_online = read(dir, "src/client.ts").is_some_and(|client| client.contains("writes: \"online\""));
        let matched = ports.into_iter().find(|&(port, web_port)| {
            [(Body::Todos, false), (Body::Home(None), false), (Body::Home(None), true)].iter()
                .any(|(body, personas)| [false, true].iter().any(|password| [false, true].iter().any(|online| {
                    let readme = starter::identity_readme(platform, &title, body, *personas, &starter::Offline::Unknown, *password);
                    let readme = if *online { starter::online_readme(&readme) } else { readme };
                    masked == starter::without_offline(&super::localize(path, &super::without_design_references(&readme), port, web_port))
                })))
        });
        let Some((port, web_port)) = matched else { modified.push(path.to_owned()); continue };
        let wanted = super::localize(path, &super::without_design_references(&starter::identity_readme(platform, &title, &wanted_body, personas, &own.offline, own.identity.is_some_and(|(password, guests)| password && !guests))), port, web_port);
        let wanted = if explicit_online { starter::online_readme(&wanted) } else { wanted };
        if wanted == text { continue; }
        let about = if todos { "the todo starter's operations, which your snapback/*.q declares again" } else { "your snapback/*.q and the Home screen, named after this directory" };
        let only_offline = !text.starts_with("# Todos") && starter::without_offline(&wanted) == masked;
        // A schema that does not compile right now says nothing about what it syncs.
        if only_offline && own.offline == starter::Offline::Unknown { continue; }
        let why = if only_offline {
            "the unmodified starter README says what works offline for another schema; adapt rewrites that paragraph from the tables your snapback/*.q syncs".to_owned()
        } else {
            format!("the unmodified starter README describes {}; adapt rewrites it to describe {about}", if text.starts_with("# Todos") { "the todo starter (its todos table and guest sign-in)" } else { "the Home screen for another schema" })
        };
        found.push(Leftover { path, why, rewrite: Some(wanted) });
    }
}

/// An Expo `app.json` that still carries the todo starter's slug, scheme and
/// bundle ids (written by 0.4.21 and earlier): adapt names them after the
/// directory and changes nothing else in the file.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — wave 6 (067): adapted Expo apps kept dev.snapback4.todos.
fn app_json(dir: &Path, found: &mut Vec<Leftover>) {
    let Some(text) = read(dir, "app.json") else { return };
    let Ok(config) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&text) else { return };
    let expo = &config["expo"];
    let [(_, slug), (_, scheme), (_, bundle)] = starter::TODO_IDS;
    if expo["slug"] != slug || expo["scheme"] != scheme || expo["ios"]["bundleIdentifier"] != bundle || expo["android"]["package"] != bundle { return; }
    let (new_slug, new_scheme, new_bundle) = starter::app_ids(&super::title(dir));
    let quoted = |value: &str| format!("\"{value}\"");
    let rewrite = text.replace(&quoted(bundle), &quoted(&new_bundle)).replace(&quoted(slug), &quoted(&new_slug)).replace(&quoted(scheme), &quoted(&new_scheme));
    found.push(Leftover { path: "app.json", why: format!("the todo starter's ids (slug {slug}, scheme {scheme}, bundle id {bundle}); adapt names them after this directory ({new_slug}, {new_scheme}, {new_bundle})"), rewrite: Some(rewrite) });
}

// Repair only the exact SVG asset and setting emitted by 0.4.26. Custom
// images/settings remain owned by the app, including other SVG paths.
fn expo_favicon(dir: &Path, found: &mut Vec<Leftover>) {
    if read(dir, "assets/favicon.svg").as_deref() != Some(include_str!("starter/favicon.svg")) { return; }
    let Some(text) = read(dir, "app.json") else { return };
    let Ok(config) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&text) else { return };
    if config.pointer("/expo/web/favicon").and_then(|value| value.as_str()) != Some("./assets/favicon.svg") { return; }
    let why = "the unmodified Expo favicon is SVG, which Expo's web export requires to be raster".to_owned();
    if let Some(existing) = found.iter_mut().find(|change| change.path == "app.json") {
        let mut config: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(existing.rewrite.as_ref().unwrap()).unwrap();
        config["expo"]["web"].as_object_mut().unwrap().remove("favicon");
        existing.rewrite = Some(super::pretty(&config));
        existing.why.push_str("; Expo's web export also requires a raster favicon");
    } else {
        let mut config = config;
        config["expo"]["web"].as_object_mut().unwrap().remove("favicon");
        found.push(Leftover { path: "app.json", why: why.clone(), rewrite: Some(super::pretty(&config)) });
    }
    found.push(Leftover { path: "assets/favicon.svg", why, rewrite: None });
}

fn number_after(text: &str, prefix: &str) -> Option<u16> {
    let at = text.find(prefix)? + prefix.len();
    let digits: String = text[at..].chars().take_while(char::is_ascii_digit).collect();
    digits.parse().ok()
}

/// The sign-in a schema admits, as the starter screen it needs: the guest
/// screen (`None`), the persona picker (`Some(personas)`), or no starter
/// screen fits (`Err(advice)`).
#[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum SignIn { Guest, Password, Personas(Vec<String>) }
impl SignIn {
    fn label(&self) -> String {
        match self { Self::Guest => "the guest sign-in".into(), Self::Password => "the password sign-in".into(), Self::Personas(names) => format!("a persona picker ({})", names.join(", ")) }
    }
    fn personas(&self) -> Option<&[String]> { match self { Self::Personas(names) => Some(names), _ => None } }
}
fn wanted(own: &OwnSchema) -> Result<SignIn, &'static str> {
    match own.identity {
        Some((_, true)) => Ok(SignIn::Guest),
        Some((true, false)) => Ok(SignIn::Password),
        None if !own.personas.is_empty() => Ok(SignIn::Personas(own.personas.clone())),
}

/// The screen a signed-in viewer sees: the starter's `Todos`, which calls
/// list/add/toggle/remove, or `Home`, which compiles against any schema (the
/// viewer and the generated api's operation names, for the project to replace),
/// with one real read when the schema has a query that takes no required
/// arguments (its `api` path, `notes` or `module.notes`).
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — a starter screen must typecheck against the schema beside it.
// @ref LLP 3000.013#6.6 — wave 5: the adapted Home renders one useQuery through matchRead, the idioms Todos taught.
#[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Debug)]
pub(super) enum Body { Todos, Home(Option<String>) }

/// The starter's screen and client for a schema: the guest sign-in, or, with
/// `personas`, the persona picker, around `body`. The texts are as the
/// starter ships them, before init's port rewrite.
fn screens_version(platform: Platform, personas: Option<&[String]>, body: &Body, previous: bool) -> (String, String) {
    let version = |current, old| if previous { old } else { current };
    let (guest, guest_client, persona, persona_client, todos, home) = match platform {
        Platform::Web => (include_str!("starter/web/guest.tsx"), version(include_str!("starter/web/client.ts"), include_str!("starter/previous/wave9/web-client.ts")), include_str!("starter/personas/web-app.tsx"), include_str!("starter/personas/web-client.ts"), version(include_str!("starter/web/todos.tsx"), include_str!("starter/previous/wave9/web-todos.tsx")), include_str!("starter/web/home.tsx")),
        Platform::Expo => (include_str!("starter/expo/guest.tsx"), version(include_str!("starter/expo/client.ts"), include_str!("starter/previous/wave9/expo-client.ts")), version(include_str!("starter/personas/expo-app.tsx"), include_str!("starter/previous/wave9/expo-personas.tsx")), include_str!("starter/personas/expo-client.ts"), version(include_str!("starter/expo/todos.tsx"), include_str!("starter/previous/wave9/expo-todos.tsx")), include_str!("starter/expo/home.tsx")),
    };
    let first_read = match platform { Platform::Web => include_str!("starter/web/first-read.tsx"), Platform::Expo => include_str!("starter/expo/first-read.tsx") };
    let (head, client) = match personas {
        None => (guest, guest_client.to_owned()),
        Some(personas) => {
            let list = personas.iter().map(|name| serde_json::to_string(name).unwrap()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
            (persona, persona_client.replace("PERSONAS", &format!("[{list}]")))
        }
    };
    let app = match body {
        Body::Todos => compose(head, todos),
        Body::Home(op) => {
            let (head, home) = if personas.is_some() { (persona_head_home(head), persona_home(home)) } else { (head.replace("<Todos />", "<Home />"), home.to_owned()) };
            match op {
                None => compose(&head, &home.replace("    <FirstRead />\n", "")),
                Some(op) => compose(&compose(&head, &home), &first_read.replace("api.OP", &format!("api.{op}"))),
            }
        }
    };
    (super::without_design_references(&app), super::without_design_references(&client))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.013.000#17 — both identity modes choose the no-device journal when nothing syncs.
pub(super) fn web_client(client: String, offline: &Offline) -> String {
    if matches!(offline, Offline::Tables { synced, .. } if synced.is_empty()) {
        client.replace("import { webDevice } from \"snapback4/web\";\n", "")
            .replace("tier === \"web\" ? webDevice() : ", "")
            .replace("device: webDevice(), ", "").replace(", device: webDevice()", "")
            .replace("each with its own device and store", "each with its own principal write journal")
            .replace("web uses the wasm device", "web uses the principal write journal (no synced tables)")
            .replace("web uses the\nwasm device", "web uses the principal write journal (no synced tables)")
    } else { client }
}

/// Explicit server execution still uses the browser journal for writes.
// @ref LLP 3000.013.000#3 — explicit online writes choose the journal even if the retained schema syncs.
pub(super) fn online_web_client(client: String) -> String {
    web_client(client, &Offline::Tables { synced: vec![], total: 0 })
        .replace("createApp({ ", "createApp({ writes: \"online\", ")
        .replace("// A device supplies offline reads for synced tables and queues writes.\n// With no synced table, init omits the device and uses the browser write journal.", "// Reads execute on the server; browser writes use the principal journal.")
}

pub(super) fn online_readme(text: &str) -> String {
    let text = text.replace("A Vite + React app over a local-first Snapback 4 backend", "A Vite + React app over an online Snapback 4 backend")
        .replace("It works offline and syncs when the link returns.", "Reads need the server; offline writes queue in the browser journal.")
        .replace("one `createApp` for the whole app. This synced todo schema\n  uses `device: webDevice()`", "one `createApp` with `writes: \"online\"` and\n  `localStorageSessionStore()`, without a device")
        .replace("one `createApp` per app (or chosen persona). With no synced\n  table it uses the browser principal journal; synced tables use `webDevice()`\n  for offline reads and predictions.", "one `createApp` per app (or chosen persona), explicitly selecting\n  `writes: \"online\"`. Reads run on the server and writes use the browser journal.")
        .replace("(tables, rules, `sync`, queries, mutations)", "(tables, rules, queries, mutations)");
    const ONLINE: &str = "Offline: reads need the server. Writes queue in this browser's principal journal\nand settle when the link returns. This starter explicitly selects online writes\nand ships no device, even if you later add a synced table.";
    let mut text = text.split("\n\n").map(|paragraph| if paragraph.starts_with("Offline") { ONLINE } else { paragraph }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n\n");
    if !text.split("\n\n").any(|paragraph| paragraph.starts_with("Offline")) {
        text = text.replacen("\n\n```sh", &format!("\n\n{ONLINE}\n\n```sh"), 1);
    }
    text
}

/// Behind the persona picker there is no session: "Sign out" (`auth.logout()`)
/// showed "Signed out" and then a "Choose a persona" button, two steps for a
/// switch (wave 5 lane 062). The persona Home takes the picker's `leave` and
/// says "Switch persona"; the guest Home keeps its sign-out.
// @ref LLP 3000.036#15.10 — the adapted persona Home switches persona; it never signs out.
fn persona_head_home(head: &str) -> String {
    let leave = ["() => setPersona(null)", "() => choose(null)"].into_iter().find(|leave| head.contains(&format!("<Account leave={{{leave}}}><Todos /></Account>")))
        .expect("the persona heads render <Account leave={…}><Todos /></Account>");
    head.replace(&format!("<Account leave={{{leave}}}><Todos /></Account>"), &format!("<Account leave={{{leave}}}><Home leave={{{leave}}} /></Account>"))
}

fn persona_home(home: &str) -> String {
    let replacements = [
        ("function Home() {", "function Home({ leave }: { leave: () => void }) {"),
        ("  const auth = useAuth(), link = useLink();", "  const link = useLink();"),
        (r#"<Button title="Sign out" onPress={() => void auth.logout()} />"#, r#"<Button title="Switch persona" onPress={leave} />"#),
        (r#"<button onClick={() => void auth.logout()}>Sign out</button>"#, r#"<button onClick={leave}>Switch persona</button>"#),
        ("// Never null here: Account renders this screen only once signed in.", "// Never null here: Account renders this screen only once the persona's app is open."),
    ];
    let mut home = home.to_owned();
    for (from, to) in replacements { home = home.replace(from, to); }
    assert!(!home.contains("auth.") && home.contains("Switch persona"), "the persona Home has no session to sign out of");
    home.replace("import { useAuth, useLink, useSignedInViewer }", "import { useLink, useSignedInViewer }")
}

/// One module from a sign-in head and a screen body, each written as a whole
/// file: their single-line named imports merge per module (packages before
/// `../`, then `./`), then the head's code, then the body's.
fn compose(head: &str, body: &str) -> String {
    fn split(text: &str) -> (Vec<&str>, &str) {
        let mut imports = Vec::new();
        let mut rest = text;
        while let Some(line) = rest.split_inclusive('\n').next().filter(|line| line.starts_with("import ")) {
            imports.push(line.trim_end());
            rest = &rest[line.len()..];
        }
        (imports, rest.trim_start_matches('\n'))
    }
    let (head_imports, head_code) = split(head);
    let (body_imports, body_code) = split(body);
    // (`import {` or `import type {`, module, names) in first-seen order.
    let mut merged: Vec<(String, String, Vec<String>)> = Vec::new();
    for line in head_imports.into_iter().chain(body_imports) {
        let (Some(open), Some(close)) = (line.find('{'), line.find('}')) else { panic!("starter imports are `import {{ … }} from \"…\";`: {line}") };
        let (kind, module) = (line[..open].trim().to_owned(), line[close + 1..].trim().to_owned());
        let names = line[open + 1..close].split(',').map(|name| name.trim().to_owned()).filter(|name| !name.is_empty());
        match merged.iter_mut().find(|(k, m, _)| *k == kind && *m == module) {
            Some((_, _, existing)) => for name in names { if !existing.contains(&name) { existing.push(name); } },
            None => merged.push((kind, module, names.collect())),
        }
    }
    merged.sort_by_key(|(_, module, _)| if module.contains("\"../") { 1 } else if module.contains("\"./") { 2 } else { 0 });
    let imports = merged.iter().map(|(kind, module, names)| format!("{kind} {{ {} }} {module}\n", names.join(", "))).collect::<String>();
    format!("{imports}\n{head_code}\n{body_code}")
}

const GUEST_SIGN_IN: &str = "Choose \"Continue as a guest\" to sign in (`use identity`); `npx snapback4 guide personas`\nadds password accounts and development personas.";
const PERSONA_SIGN_IN: &str = "The first screen (on the web also `?as=<name>`) acts as a persona your schema declares with\n`use personas`: `usePersonaApp` opens one app per persona and closes the old one on a switch.\nPersonas act only on `snapback4 dev`: add `use identity` and a sign-in screen before you\ndeploy (`npx snapback4 guide personas`).";

/// The starter README for a screen: the Todos README describes the starter's
/// own schema and screen; beside a project's schema (the neutral `Home`) the
/// README is named after the directory and describes that schema's sign-in.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — wave 6 (042): an adapted starter kept a README about todos and guest sign-in.
pub(super) fn readme(platform: Platform, title: &str, body: &Body, personas: bool, offline: &Offline) -> String {
    let todos = match platform { Platform::Web => include_str!("starter/web/README.md"), Platform::Expo => include_str!("starter/expo/README.md") };
    if *body == Body::Todos {
        return todos.replace("native device and work offline;", "native device;")
            .replace("wasm device.\n", &format!("wasm device.\n\n{}\n", offline.paragraph(platform)));
    }
    let home = match platform { Platform::Web => include_str!("starter/web/README.home.md"), Platform::Expo => include_str!("starter/expo/README.home.md") };
    let text = home.replacen("# TITLE", &format!("# {title}"), 1).replace("SIGN_IN", if personas { PERSONA_SIGN_IN } else { GUEST_SIGN_IN })
        .replacen("OFFLINE\n", &format!("{}\n", offline.paragraph(platform)), 1);
    if platform == Platform::Web { return text; }
    // The Expo toolchain notes are the Todos README's own, word for word.
    let advisories = todos.split_once("\n> ").map(|(_, rest)| format!("> {}", rest.split_once("\n\n").expect("the advisory box ends").0)).expect("the Expo README has its advisory box");

    };
    let shape = starter::Shape {
        title: title(dir),
        password: own.as_ref().is_some_and(|own| own.identity.is_some_and(|(password, guests)| password && !guests)),
        personas: own.as_ref().filter(|own| own.identity.is_none() && !own.personas.is_empty()).map(|own| own.personas.clone()),
        body: match own.as_ref() { Some(own) if !adapt::fits_todos(own) => starter::Body::Home(own.reads.first().cloned()), _ => starter::Body::Todos },
        offline: own.as_ref().map(|own| own.offline.clone()).unwrap_or_else(|| starter::Offline::Tables { synced: vec!["todos".into()], total: 1 }),
    };
    let home = matches!(shape.body, starter::Body::Home(_));
    let mut files = if social { social::files() }
        else if chat && web { web::files() } else if chat { expo_files() }
        else if web { starter::web_files(&shape) } else if expo { starter::expo_files(&shape) }
        else if online { online_files() } else { vec![("snapback/schema.q", snapback4_lang::guide::TEMPLATE.to_owned())] };
    if online_web {
        for (name, text) in &mut files {
            if *name == "src/client.ts" { *text = starter::online_web_client(text.clone()); }
            if *name == "README.md" { *text = starter::online_readme(text); }
            if *name == "snapback/schema.q" { *text = text.replace("  sync to .ownerId            -- each owner's devices hold their own todos and work offline", "  online only"); }
        }
    }
    if web && !files.iter().any(|(name, _)| *name == "public/favicon.svg") {
        files.push(("public/favicon.svg", include_str!("init/starter/favicon.svg").to_owned()));
        for (name, text) in &mut files { if *name == "index.html" { *text = text.replace("<head>", "<head>\n<link rel=\"icon\" href=\"/favicon.svg\" type=\"image/svg+xml\" />"); } }
    }
    // The starter's rows and cases test its own todos schema; beside a

// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#3 — generated proofs use the published kit and runtime ports.
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use super::{Options, pretty};

pub(super) fn readme(mut text: String) -> String {
    if !text.contains("`npm run frontend:proof`") {
        text.push_str("\n## Frontend and day-two proofs\n\nAfter `npm run build` (web) or `npm run build:web` (Expo), run\n`npm run frontend:proof` to prove sign-in and the starter form in Chromium.\nAfter replacing the screen, add your business assertions to scripts/frontend-proof.mjs.\n`npm run day2:proof -- versions/v1.q versions/v2.q` proves saved-source deploy,\nrollback and compatible forward without replacing your working schema.\nSet `PORT_LO` and `PORT_HI` at run time to confine proof servers and launchers;\nunset means allocated proof ports and the interactive starter defaults.\nSet `CHROME=/path/to/chromium` or install Playwright's Chromium (`npx playwright-core install chromium`).\n");
    }
    text
}

pub(super) fn files(files: &mut Vec<(&'static str, String)>, _options: &Options, backend: u16, frontend: u16, web: bool, _shape: &super::starter::Shape) {
    for (name, text) in files.iter_mut().filter(|(name, _)| *name == "README.md") {
        *text = readme(std::mem::take(text)); let _ = name;
    }
    let proof = include_str!("frontend-proof.mjs").to_owned();
    files.push(("scripts/frontend-proof.mjs", proof));
    files.push(("scripts/day2-proof.mjs", include_str!("day2-proof.mjs").to_owned()));
    files.push(("scripts/launch.mjs", include_str!("launch.mjs").replace("BACKEND", &backend.to_string()).replace("FRONTEND", &frontend.to_string())));
    for (name, text) in files.iter_mut().filter(|(name, _)| *name == "package.json") {
        let mut package: Value = serde_json::from_str(text).unwrap();
        package["scripts"]["frontend:proof"] = json!("node scripts/frontend-proof.mjs");
        package["scripts"]["day2:proof"] = json!("node scripts/day2-proof.mjs");
        package["scripts"]["proof:web"] = json!("node scripts/frontend-proof.mjs");
        package["devDependencies"]["playwright-core"] = json!("1.55.1");
        package["scripts"]["backend"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs backend");
        if web {
            package["scripts"]["dev"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs vite");
            package["scripts"]["preview"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs vite-preview");
        } else {
            package["scripts"]["start"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs expo");
            package["scripts"]["web"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs expo-web");
            package["scripts"]["native:smoke"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs native-smoke");
        }
        *text = pretty(&package);
        let _ = name;
    }
}

/// Adapt adds missing proof infrastructure, preserving files and script names
/// already owned by the application. It never runs a package manager.
pub(super) fn plan(dir: &std::path::Path, own: &super::OwnSchema) -> std::io::Result<Vec<(&'static str, String)>> {
    let web = dir.join("src/App.tsx").is_file();
    if !web && !dir.join("App.tsx").is_file() { return Ok(Vec::new()); }
    let shape = super::starter::Shape { title: super::title(dir), password: own.identity.is_some_and(|(p, g)| p && !g),
        personas: own.identity.is_none().then(|| own.personas.clone()).filter(|p| !p.is_empty()),
        body: super::starter::Body::Home(None), offline: own.offline.clone() };
    let path = dir.join("package.json");
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path)?;
    let mut package: Value = serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(std::io::Error::other)?;
    let original = package.clone();
    let mut generated = vec![("package.json", pretty(&package))];
    files(&mut generated, &Options { positionals: vec![], flags: Default::default(), json: false }, 4400, if web { 5173 } else { 8081 }, web, &shape);
    let wanted: Value = serde_json::from_str(&generated[0].1).unwrap();
    for group in ["scripts", "devDependencies"] {
        if !package[group].is_object() { package[group] = json!({}); }
        for (key, value) in wanted[group].as_object().unwrap() {
            if (key == "frontend:proof" || key == "day2:proof" || key == "playwright-core") && package[group].get(key).is_none() { package[group][key] = value.clone(); }
        }
    }
    let mut additions: Vec<_> = generated.into_iter().skip(1)
        .filter(|(name, _)| !dir.join(name).exists()).collect();
    if package != original { additions.push(("package.json", pretty(&package))); }
    Ok(additions)
}

pub(super) fn apply(dir: &std::path::Path, additions: &[(&str, String)]) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    for (name, body) in additions {
        let path = dir.join(name);
        if *name == "package.json" { super::replace_file(&path, body)?; }
        else {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap())?;
            use std::io::Write;
            let mut file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new().write(true).create_new(true).open(&path)?;
            file.write_all(body.as_bytes())?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

    text.replacen("ADVISORIES", &advisories, 1).replacen("TOOLCHAIN\n", &toolchain, 1)
}

/// The Expo app's `app.json` ids, named after the directory's title: a slug
/// (`bake-sale`), a URL scheme and a bundle id that start with a letter
/// (`bakesale`, `dev.snapback4.bakesale`).
pub(super) fn app_ids(title: &str) -> (String, String, String) {
    let words: Vec<String> = title.split(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_alphanumeric()).filter(|word| !word.is_empty()).map(str::to_ascii_lowercase).collect();
    let slug = if words.is_empty() { "app".to_owned() } else { words.join("-") };
    let mut scheme = words.concat();
    if !scheme.starts_with(|c: char| c.is_ascii_alphabetic()) { scheme = format!("app{scheme}"); }
    let bundle = format!("dev.snapback4.{scheme}");
    (slug, scheme, bundle)
}

/// What 0.4.21 and earlier wrote into every Expo starter's `app.json`.
pub(super) const TODO_IDS: [(&str, &str); 3] = [("slug", "snapback4-todos"), ("scheme", "snapback4todos"), ("bundle", "dev.snapback4.todos")];

pub(super) fn web_files(shape: &Shape) -> Vec<(&'static str, String)> {
    Some(op.to_owned())
}

fn read(dir: &Path, path: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let path = dir.join(path);
    path.symlink_metadata().ok().filter(|metadata| metadata.is_file())?;
    std::fs::read_to_string(path).ok()
}

/// A starter client without what init or the schema varies: the backend port
/// and the persona list.
fn normalized(client: &str) -> String {
    let lines = client.split_inclusive('\n').map(|line| {
        if line.starts_with(PERSONAS) { format!("{PERSONAS}[];\n") } else { line.to_owned() }
    }).collect::<String>();
    match port_of(&lines) { Some(port) => lines.replace(&format!("127.0.0.1:{port}"), "127.0.0.1:4400"), None => lines }
}

const PERSONAS: &str = "export const personas: readonly string[] = ";

fn personas_of(client: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    client.lines().find_map(|line| line.strip_prefix(PERSONAS))
        .and_then(|list| serde_json::from_str(list.trim_end().trim_end_matches(';')).ok()).unwrap_or_default()
}

fn port_of(client: &str) -> Option<&str> {
    let at = client.find("127.0.0.1:")? + "127.0.0.1:".len();
    let digits = client[at..].find(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_digit()).unwrap_or(client.len() - at);
    (digits > 0).then(|| &client[at..at + digits])
}

/// One `check` line, or none: the leftovers and the command that fixes them.
pub(crate) fn report(dir: &Path) -> Option<String> {
    let (found, advice) = leftovers(dir);
    if found.is_empty() && advice.is_empty() { return None; }
    let mut lines = Vec::new();
    for leftover in &found { lines.push(format!("  {}: {}", leftover.path, leftover.why)); }
    for line in &advice { lines.push(format!("  {line}")); }
    if !found.is_empty() {
        lines.push("  fix: snapback4 init --adapt (removes or rewrites only these files, which still match the starter; your own files are never touched)".into());
    }
    Some(format!("starter: {} left from `snapback4 init` that {} not fit your schema\n{}",
        if found.is_empty() { "a screen".to_owned() } else { format!("{} file{}", found.len(), if found.len() == 1 { "" } else { "s" }) },
        if found.len() == 1 || found.is_empty() { "does" } else { "do" }, lines.join("\n")))
}

/// The same, for `check --json`: `null` when nothing is left over.
pub(crate) fn report_json(dir: &Path) -> serde_json::Value {
    let (found, advice) = leftovers(dir);
    if found.is_empty() && advice.is_empty() { return serde_json::Value::Null; }
    serde_json::json!({
        "files": found.iter().map(|leftover| serde_json::json!({ "path": leftover.path, "why": leftover.why, "adapt": if leftover.rewrite.is_some() { "rewrite" } else { "remove" } })).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        "advice": advice,
        "fix": if found.is_empty() { serde_json::Value::Null } else { "snapback4 init --adapt".into() },
    })
}

/// `snapback4 init --adapt [dir]`.
pub(super) fn adapt(dir: &Path, dry_run: bool, apply: bool) -> i32 {
    let own = match own_schema(dir) {
        Ok(Some(own)) => own,
        Ok(None) => { eprintln!("init --adapt: no snapback/*.q in {}; nothing to adapt to (run `snapback4 init --web` or `--expo` first)", dir.display()); return 1; }
        Err(error) => { eprintln!("init: {error}"); return 2; }
    };
    let (found, advice, modified) = plan(dir);
    let proof = match super::proof::plan(dir, &own) {
        Ok(proof) => proof,
        Err(error) => { eprintln!("init --adapt proof: {error}"); return 2; }
    };
    if !found.is_empty() || !proof.is_empty() {
        println!("init --adapt: planned changes (only unmodified starter files):");
        for change in &found { println!("  {} {} — {}", if change.rewrite.is_some() { "rewrite" } else { "remove" }, change.path, change.why); }
    }
    for (path, _) in &proof {
        println!("  {} {} — missing proof infrastructure", if *path == "package.json" { "extend" } else { "add" }, path);
    }
    if !modified.is_empty() { println!("init --adapt: application-owned or modified files stay unchanged: {}", modified.join(", ")); }
    if dry_run || (!apply && !modified.is_empty() && (!found.is_empty() || !proof.is_empty())) {
        println!("init --adapt: preview only; no files changed. Run `snapback4 init{} --adapt --apply` to apply the listed changes", if dir == Path::new(".") { String::new() } else { format!(" {}", dir.display()) });
        for line in advice { println!("init --adapt: {line}"); }
        return 0;
    }
    if let Err(error) = super::proof::apply(dir, &proof) { eprintln!("init --adapt proof: {error}"); return 2; }
    let mut removed = Vec::new();
    let mut rewritten = Vec::new();
    for leftover in &found {
        let path = dir.join(leftover.path);
        let result = match &leftover.rewrite {
            Some(text) => super::replace_file(&path, text).map(|()| rewritten.push(leftover.path)),
            None => std::fs::remove_file(&path).map(|()| removed.push(leftover.path)),
        };
        if let Err(error) = result { eprintln!("init --adapt: {}: {error}", path.display()); return 2; }
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — adapt removes the files it names, never a directory: the next step writes fixtures/<table>.jsonl.
    if found.is_empty() && proof.is_empty() { println!("init --adapt: nothing to adapt — no unmodified starter file conflicts with your snapback/*.q"); }
    if !removed.is_empty() {
        let kept = if removed.iter().any(|path| path.starts_with("fixtures/")) { "; fixtures/ stays for your own rows" } else { "" };
        println!("init --adapt: removed {} (unmodified starter files{kept}; write your own as fixtures/<table>.jsonl and fixtures/test.cases.jsonl, `snapback4 guide testing`)", removed.join(" and "));
    }
    if !rewritten.is_empty() {
        let why = found.iter().find(|leftover| leftover.rewrite.is_some()).map(|leftover| leftover.why.as_str()).unwrap_or_default();
        println!("init --adapt: rewrote {} — {why}", rewritten.join(" and "));
    }
    if found.iter().any(|leftover| leftover.rewrite.as_deref().is_some_and(|text| text.contains("function Home("))) {
        println!("init --adapt: next: `snapback4 check` writes snapback/generated/api.ts for the Home screen; replace Home with your own screens (`snapback4 guide client`)");
    }
    for line in advice { println!("init --adapt: {line}"); }
    0
}

#[test]
fn untouched_wave9_screens_still_adapt_and_edited_clients_stay_owned() {
    for platform in [Platform::Web, Platform::Expo] {
        for password in [false, true] {
            let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-w10-adapt-{}-{platform:?}-{password}", std::process::id()));
            std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("snapback")).unwrap(); std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("src")).unwrap();
            let schema = include_str!("starter/schema.q").replace("use identity ", "use identity password ");
            std::fs::write(dir.join("snapback/schema.q"), schema).unwrap();
            let (app, client) = starter::previous_screens(platform, None, &Body::Todos, password);
            let (app_path, client_path) = platform.paths();
            std::fs::write(dir.join(app_path), &app).unwrap(); std::fs::write(dir.join(client_path), &client).unwrap();
            let (changes, _) = leftovers(&dir);
            assert!(changes.iter().any(|change| change.path == app_path), "{platform:?}, password={password}: previous screens recognized");
            std::fs::write(dir.join(client_path), format!("{client}\n// application-owned edit\n")).unwrap();
            let (changes, _) = leftovers(&dir);
            assert!(!changes.iter().any(|change| change.path == app_path || change.path == client_path), "edited pair stays owned");
            std::fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
        }
    }
}
//! Init's explicit online web tier and adaptation preview contract.
use std::{fs, path::PathBuf, process::{Command, Output}, sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering}};
const BIN: &str = env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_snapback4");
static NEXT: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(0);
struct Project(PathBuf);
impl Project {
    fn new() -> Self {
        let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-dx3-init-{}-{}", std::process::id(), NEXT.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed)));
        fs::create_dir_all(&path).unwrap(); Self(path)
    }
    fn run(&self, args: &[&str]) -> Output {
        Command::new(BIN).args(args).current_dir(&self.0).env_remove("PORT_LO").env_remove("PORT_HI").output().unwrap()
    }
    fn text(&self, path: &str) -> String { fs::read_to_string(self.0.join(path)).unwrap() }
}
impl Drop for Project { fn drop(&mut self) { fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0).unwrap(); } }
fn ok(output: Output) -> String {
    assert!(output.status.success(), "{output:?}"); String::from_utf8(output.stdout).unwrap()
}
#[test]
fn explicit_online_web_starter_checks_and_keeps_the_choice_when_adapted() {
    let dir = Project::new();
    let output = ok(dir.run(&["init", "--web", "--online"]));
    assert!(output.contains("no device"), "{output}");
    assert!(dir.text("src/client.ts").contains("writes: \"online\""));
    assert!(!dir.text("src/client.ts").contains("webDevice"));
    assert!(!dir.text("snapback/schema.q").contains("sync to"));
    assert!(!dir.text("README.md").contains("works offline"));
    ok(dir.run(&["check"])); ok(dir.run(&["test", "--fixture", "fixtures", "--as", "alice"]));
    let schema = dir.text("snapback/schema.q").replace("use identity ", "use identity password ").replace("  online only", "  sync to .ownerId");
    fs::write(dir.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), schema).unwrap();
    let plan = ok(dir.run(&["init", "--adapt", "--dry-run"]));
    assert!(plan.contains("rewrite src/App.tsx") && plan.contains("preview only"), "{plan}");
    ok(dir.run(&["init", "--adapt"]));
    assert!(dir.text("src/App.tsx").contains("auth.signup"));
    assert!(dir.text("src/client.ts").contains("writes: \"online\"") && !dir.text("src/client.ts").contains("webDevice"));
    assert!(dir.text("README.md").contains("ships no device"));
    ok(dir.run(&["check"]));
}
#[test]
fn online_web_keeps_an_existing_synced_schema_and_supports_personas() {
    for identity in ["use personas alice", "use identity password"] {
        let dir = Project::new(); fs::create_dir(dir.0.join("snapback")).unwrap();
        let schema = format!("{identity}\ntable notes:\n  owner: principal\n  title: text <=80\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  sync to .owner\nquery mine():\n  return notes where .owner = viewer first 20 by .id\nmutation add(title: text <=80):\n  return insert notes {{ owner: viewer, title }}\n");
        fs::write(dir.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), &schema).unwrap();
        ok(dir.run(&["init", "--web", "--online"]));
        assert_eq!(dir.text("snapback/schema.q"), schema);
        assert!(dir.text("src/client.ts").contains("writes: \"online\""));
        assert!(!dir.text("src/client.ts").contains("webDevice"));
        ok(dir.run(&["check"]));
    }
}
#[test]
fn dry_run_lists_removals_and_rewrites_without_changing_files() {
    let dir = Project::new(); ok(dir.run(&["init", "--web"]));
    fs::write(dir.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), "use personas alice\ntable notes:\n  body: text <=80\n  public 'shared'\n").unwrap();
    let app = dir.text("src/App.tsx"); let readme = dir.text("README.md"); let fixture = dir.text("fixtures/todos.jsonl");
    let plan = ok(dir.run(&["init", "--adapt", "--dry-run"]));
    assert!(plan.contains("remove fixtures/todos.jsonl") && plan.contains("rewrite src/App.tsx"), "{plan}");
    assert_eq!(dir.text("src/App.tsx"), app); assert_eq!(dir.text("README.md"), readme); assert_eq!(dir.text("fixtures/todos.jsonl"), fixture);
    fs::write(dir.0.join("README.md"), "Application-owned introduction\n").unwrap();
    let applied = ok(dir.run(&["init", "--adapt", "--apply"]));
    assert!(applied.contains("application-owned or modified files stay unchanged: README.md"), "{applied}");
    assert_eq!(dir.text("README.md"), "Application-owned introduction\n");
    assert!(!dir.0.join("fixtures/todos.jsonl").exists());
    assert!(dir.text("src/App.tsx").contains("usePersonaApp"));
}
#[test]
fn preview_flags_require_adapt_and_cannot_be_combined() {
    for args in [vec!["init", "--dry-run"], vec!["init", "--apply"], vec!["init", "--adapt", "--dry-run", "--apply"], vec!["init", "--expo", "--online"], vec!["init", "--web", "--online", "--template", "chat"]] {
        let dir = Project::new(); let result = dir.run(&args);
        assert_eq!(result.status.code(), Some(2), "{args:?}: {result:?}");
        assert_eq!(fs::read_dir(&dir.0).unwrap().count(), 0);
    }
}

#[test]
fn proof_additions_share_the_complete_nonmutating_adaptation_plan() {
    let dir = Project::new(); ok(dir.run(&["init", "--web"]));
    for file in ["scripts/frontend-proof.mjs", "scripts/day2-proof.mjs"] { fs::remove_file(dir.0.join(file)).unwrap(); }
    let mut package: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&dir.text("package.json")).unwrap();
    for key in ["frontend:proof", "day2:proof"] { package["scripts"].as_object_mut().unwrap().remove(key); }
    package["devDependencies"].as_object_mut().unwrap().remove("playwright-core");
    fs::write(dir.0.join("package.json"), serde_json::to_string_pretty(&package).unwrap()).unwrap();
    fs::write(dir.0.join("README.md"), "Application-owned introduction\n").unwrap();
    let before = dir.text("package.json");
    for args in [vec!["init", "--adapt", "--dry-run"], vec!["init", "--adapt"]] {
        let output = ok(dir.run(&args));
        assert!(output.contains("add scripts/frontend-proof.mjs") && output.contains("add scripts/day2-proof.mjs") && output.contains("extend package.json") && output.contains("preview only"), "{output}");
        assert_eq!(dir.text("package.json"), before);
        assert!(!dir.0.join("scripts/frontend-proof.mjs").exists());
        assert_eq!(dir.text("README.md"), "Application-owned introduction\n");
    }
    ok(dir.run(&["init", "--adapt", "--apply"]));
    let package: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&dir.text("package.json")).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(package["scripts"]["frontend:proof"], "node scripts/frontend-proof.mjs");
    assert_eq!(package["devDependencies"]["playwright-core"], "1.55.1");
    assert!(dir.0.join("scripts/day2-proof.mjs").exists());
    assert_eq!(dir.text("README.md"), "Application-owned introduction\n");
}

#[test]
fn every_frontend_template_documents_proofs_and_expo_uses_the_runtime_api() {
    for args in [vec!["init", "--web"], vec!["init", "--web", "--online"], vec!["init", "--expo"], vec!["init", "--web", "--template", "chat"], vec!["init", "--expo", "--template", "chat"], vec!["init", "--template", "social"]] {
        let dir = Project::new(); ok(dir.run(&args));
        let readme = dir.text("README.md");
        for word in ["frontend:proof", "day2:proof", "PORT_LO", "PORT_HI", "CHROME"] { assert!(readme.contains(word), "{args:?}: missing {word}"); }
        let package: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&dir.text("package.json")).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(package["scripts"]["frontend:proof"], "node scripts/frontend-proof.mjs");
        if args.contains(&"--web") { assert!(dir.text("src/client.ts").contains("new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get(\"api\")")); }
        else {
            assert!(dir.text("src/client.ts").contains("Platform.OS === \"web\" ? new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get(\"api\") : null"));
            assert_eq!(package["scripts"]["native:smoke"], "node scripts/launch.mjs native-smoke");
        }
    }
}
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#9 — one range localizes every starter launcher before any file is written.
use super::super::Options;
use serde_json::{Value, json};

pub(super) fn range(options: &Options) -> Result<Option<(u16, u16)>, String> {
    // Environment belongs to the process launch, never to generated files.
    // Validate malformed assigned ranges at run time through snapback4/proof.
    let text = options.flags.get("port-range").cloned();
    let Some(text) = text else { return Ok(None) };
    let (low, high) = text.split_once('-').ok_or("--port-range must be A-B (1 <= A <= B <= 65535)")?;
    let (low, high) = (low.parse::<u16>(), high.parse::<u16>());
    match (low, high) {
        (Ok(low), Ok(high)) if low > 0 && low <= high => Ok(Some((low, high))),
        _ => Err("--port-range must be A-B (1 <= A <= B <= 65535)".into()),
    }
}

pub(super) fn scripts(files: &mut Vec<(&'static str, String)>, backend: u16, frontend: u16, range: Option<(u16, u16)>, web: bool) {
    for (name, text) in files.iter_mut() {
        if *name != "package.json" { continue; }
        let mut package: Value = serde_json::from_str(text).unwrap();
        package["scripts"]["backend"] = json!(format!("snapback4 dev --port {backend}"));
        if web {
            package["scripts"]["dev"] = json!(format!("vite --port {frontend} --strictPort"));
            package["scripts"]["preview"] = json!(format!("vite preview --port {frontend} --strictPort"));
        } else {
            package["scripts"]["start"] = json!(format!("expo start --port {frontend}"));
            package["scripts"]["web"] = json!(format!("expo start --web --port {frontend}"));
            package["scripts"]["native:smoke"] = json!(format!("snapback4 witness ios --build . --smoke --port {backend}"));
        }
        package["scripts"]["proof:web"] = json!(format!("snapback4 witness web dist{}", range.map(|(low, high)| format!(" --port-range {low}-{high}")).unwrap_or_default()));
        *text = super::pretty(&package);
    }
}

/// A README with its offline paragraph replaced by `OFFLINE`, and 0.4.22's
/// claim in the opening paragraph rewritten to today's opening, so `init
/// --adapt` recognizes an unmodified starter README whatever its schema
/// synced when it was written.
pub(super) fn without_offline(text: &str) -> String {
    let legacy = [
        ("the Snapback 4 web starter. It works offline and syncs when the link returns.\n", "the Snapback 4 web starter.\n\nOFFLINE\n"),
        ("started from the Snapback 4 Expo starter. Development and production builds\nopen the native device and work offline; Expo Go uses the online client; web\nuses the wasm device.\n",
            "started from the Snapback 4 Expo starter. Development and production builds\nopen the native device; Expo Go uses the online client; web uses the wasm\ndevice.\n\nOFFLINE\n"),
    ];
    let mut text = text.to_owned();
    for (old, new) in legacy { text = text.replacen(old, new, 1); }
    text = text.replace("web uses the principal write journal (no synced tables)", "web uses the wasm\ndevice")
        .replace("or the principal write journal (web, no synced tables)", "or `webDevice()` (web)")
        .replace("native device and work offline;", "native device;")
        .replace("web uses the wasm\ndevice", "web uses the wasm device")
        .replace("web uses the\nwasm device", "web uses the wasm device");
    let mut masked = text.split("\n\n").map(|paragraph| if paragraph.starts_with("Offline") { "OFFLINE" } else { paragraph }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n\n");
    if masked.starts_with("# Todos") && !masked.contains("\n\nOFFLINE\n\n") {
        masked = masked.replacen("wasm device.\n\n", "wasm device.\n\nOFFLINE\n\n", 1);
    }
    masked
}

/// Which starter screen pair: the guest sign-in, or the persona picker.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Debug)]
pub(super) enum Platform { Web, Expo }

# Starter Web

A Vite + React app over the Snapback 4 backend in `snapback/*.q`, started from
the Snapback 4 web starter.

Offline: no table in `snapback/*.q` declares `sync`, so reads need the server.
Writes made offline are kept in this browser's principal journal and sent when
the link returns. Add `sync` to a table to read it offline
(`npx snapback4 guide sync`).

```sh
npm install
npx snapback4 check      # compiles snapback/*.q, writes snapback/generated/api.ts
npx snapback4 dev        # serves http://127.0.0.1:4400 and reloads on save
npm run dev              # in a second terminal: http://localhost:5173
```

Choose "Continue as a guest" to sign in (`use identity`); `npx snapback4 guide personas`
adds password accounts and development personas.

## Files

- `snapback/*.q`: the backend (tables, rules, `sync`, queries, mutations).
  Edit it and `snapback4 dev` re-adopts it; `snapback4 check` regenerates the API.
- `fixtures/`: your rows as `fixtures/<table>.jsonl` and expectations in
  `fixtures/test.cases.jsonl`, for `npx snapback4 test --fixture fixtures`
  (`npx snapback4 guide testing`).
- `src/client.ts`: one `createApp` per app (or chosen persona). With no synced
  table it uses the browser principal journal; synced tables use `webDevice()`
  for offline reads and predictions. A bare browser `createClient()` refuses
  durable mutations with `E_ADMISSION`.
- `src/App.tsx`: the sign-in and `Home`, a screen to replace. It shows the
  viewer, your operations and your first query without required arguments,
  rendered with `matchRead`. Read with `useQuery(api.op, args)` or
  `usePage(api.list, args)` (a query that takes `c: cursor ?`), write with
  `useMutation(api.op)` (`npx snapback4 guide client`).
- `src/styles.css`, `index.html` (titled after this directory) and
  `vite.config.mts`: yours to change.

## Next steps

- Shapes most apps need (roles, two parties, booking, schedules):
  `npx snapback4 guide recipes`; one screen of forms: `guide cheatsheet`.
- Ship: `VITE_SNAPBACK_URL=https://your-backend npm run build` builds `dist/`
  with an offline app shell (`snapback4 web-shell dist`); serve it over HTTPS.
- A complete multi-screen example: `npx snapback4 init <dir> --web --template chat`.
use identity guests
table notes:
  owner: principal
  title: text <=80
  read <- .owner = viewer
  insert <- .owner = viewer
  online only
query mine():
  return notes where .owner = viewer first 20 by .id
mutation add(title: text <=80):
  return insert notes { owner: viewer, title }

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { spawn, spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { once } from 'node:events';
import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync, existsSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { hangBudget } from './helpers/wait.mjs';

const root = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../../', import.meta.url));
const binary = process.env.SNAPBACK4_BIN ?? join(root, 'target/debug/snapback4');
function fixture(t) {
  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'sb4-narr-')); mkdirSync(join(dir, 'snapback'));
  t.after(() => rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }));
  const deployment = join(dir, 'deployment'), schema = join(dir, 'snapback/schema.q');
  const invoke = (...args) => spawnSync(binary, args, { cwd: dir, encoding: 'utf8', timeout: hangBudget(), env: { ...process.env, PORT_LO: undefined, PORT_HI: undefined } });
  const run = (...args) => { const result = invoke(...args); assert.equal(result.status, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr); return result; };
  return { dir, deployment, schema, run, invoke };
}
import { runtimeChanges, lockedVersion, compareVersions } from '../bin/release-changes.js';
const installed = JSON.parse(readFileSync(new URL('../package.json', import.meta.url))).version;
const v1 = `use identity guests
table notes:
  body: text 1..80
  status: enum('draft') default 'draft'
  public
  insert <- allow
  update <- allow
  online only
mutation add(body: text 1..80):
  return insert notes {body}
query readNote(note: notes):
  return notes[note]
query guardedNote(note: notes):
  n = notes[note]! else MISSING_NOTE
  return n
job remind():
  chunk n in notes first 10 by byId:
    update notes[n.id] {body: n.body}
schedule remindDaily every 1d: remind
`;
test('first check recovers older versions from locks/generated metadata and matches the packaged changelog', t => {
  for (const [name, contents] of [
    ['package-lock.json', JSON.stringify({ packages: { 'node_modules/snapback4': { version: '0.4.25' } } })],
    ['bun.lock', '{"packages":{"snapback4":["snapback4@0.4.25","",{},"sri"],},}'],
    ['pnpm-lock.yaml', 'importers:\n  .:\n    dependencies:\n      snapback4:\n        specifier: ^0.4.24\n        version: 0.4.25'],
    ['snapback/generated/package.json', '{"type":"module","snapback4Version":"0.4.25"}'],
  ]) {
    const { dir, schema, run } = fixture(t); writeFileSync(schema, v1);
    mkdirSync(join(dir, 'snapback/generated'), { recursive: true }); writeFileSync(join(dir, name), contents);
    const checked = run('check', '--json'); const facts = JSON.parse(checked.stdout); assert.ok(facts.ok);
    const expected = runtimeChanges(readFileSync(new URL('../CHANGELOG.md', import.meta.url), 'utf8'), '0.4.25', installed);
    assert.ok(checked.stderr.includes(expected), checked.stderr);
    assert.match(expected, /0\.4\.26[\s\S]*run\(\).*sent \| failed/);
    assert.match(expected, /0\.4\.27/);
    const again = run('check'); assert.doesNotMatch(again.stdout + again.stderr, /runtime changes|baseline unavailable/);
  }
  const { run } = fixture(t); const guide = run('guide', 'changes').stdout;
  assert.ok(guide.includes(`Changes in ${installed}`)); assert.match(guide, /waitSeconds: 0/);
  if (installed === '0.4.27') assert.doesNotMatch(guide, /## 0\.4\.28/);
  assert.equal(lockedVersion('snapback4@0.4.25\nsnapback4@0.4.26'), undefined);
  assert.equal(lockedVersion('snapback4@0.4.25.1'), undefined);
  assert.equal(lockedVersion('importers:\n  one:\n    snapback4:\n      version: 0.4.25\n  two:\n    snapback4:\n      version: 0.4.27'), undefined);
  const human = fixture(t); writeFileSync(human.schema, v1);
  writeFileSync(join(human.dir, 'package-lock.json'), JSON.stringify({ dependencies: { snapback4: { version: '0.4.25' } } }));
  assert.match(human.run('check').stdout, /runtime changes: 0\.4\.25 →/);
});

test('runtime history remains visible when the first check refuses device preflight', t => {
  const { dir, schema, invoke } = fixture(t); writeFileSync(schema, v1);
  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'package-lock.json'), JSON.stringify({ packages: { 'node_modules/snapback4': { version: '0.4.25' } } }));
  mkdirSync(join(dir, 'app')); writeFileSync(join(dir, 'app/app.json'), JSON.stringify({ typescript: { sources: { snapback4: '../../exact2/snapback4/ts', backend: '../snapback/generated' } } }));
  const checked = invoke('check', '--json'); assert.equal(checked.status, 1);
  assert.equal(JSON.parse(checked.stdout).denied.code, 'E_DEVICE_VERSION');
  assert.match(checked.stderr, /runtime changes: 0\.4\.25 →/); assert.match(checked.stderr, /run\(\).*sent \| failed/);
  assert.equal(existsSync(join(dir, 'snapback/generated/api.ts')), false);
  const again = invoke('check', '--json'); assert.equal(again.status, 1);
  assert.doesNotMatch(again.stdout + again.stderr, /runtime changes:/);
});


test('downgrade ordering includes prereleases and numeric prerelease identifiers', () => {
  for (const [older, newer] of [['0.4.26', '0.4.28'], ['0.4.28-rc.1', '0.4.28'], ['0.4.28-rc.2', '0.4.28-rc.10'], ['0.4.28-1', '0.4.28-a']]) {
    assert.ok(compareVersions(older, newer) < 0); assert.ok(compareVersions(newer, older) > 0);
  }
  assert.equal(compareVersions('0.4.28', '0.4.28'), 0);
});
//! One release history for the packaged card and upgrade/check interval.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#11-wave-11-runtime-upgrade-history — no compiler snapshot is required for runtime history.
pub const CHANGELOG: &str = include_str!("../../../packages/snapback4/CHANGELOG.md");

pub fn version(text: &str) -> Option<(u32, u32, u32)> {
    let mut parts = text.split('.');
    let result = (parts.next()?.parse().ok()?, parts.next()?.parse().ok()?, parts.next()?.split('-').next()?.parse().ok()?);
    parts.next().is_none().then_some(result)
}
pub fn between(history: &str, previous: Option<&str>, installed: &str) -> String {
    let Some(installed) = version(installed) else { return String::new(); };
    let previous = previous.and_then(version);
    let mut selected = false;
    let mut out = String::new();
    for line in history.lines() {
        if let Some(release) = line.strip_prefix("## ") {
            selected = version(release).is_some_and(|v| v <= installed && previous.is_none_or(|old| v > old));
        }
        if selected && line.starts_with("## ") { if !out.is_empty() { out.push('\n'); } out.push_str(line); out.push_str("\n\n"); }
        else if selected && line.starts_with("- ") { out.push_str(line); out.push('\n'); }
    }
    out
}
pub fn interval(previous: &str, installed: &str) -> String {
    if version(previous).zip(version(installed)).is_none_or(|(old, new)| old >= new) { return String::new(); }
    let entries = between(CHANGELOG, Some(previous), installed);
    if entries.is_empty() { return String::new(); }
    format!("runtime changes: {previous} → {installed}\n{entries}History begins at 0.4.26; earlier changes are not recorded.\n")
}

# Todos: a Snapback 4 web starter

A Vite + React app over a local-first Snapback 4 backend: one table, one live
query and three mutations. It works offline and syncs when the link returns.

```sh
npm install
npx snapback4 check      # compiles snapback/schema.q, writes snapback/generated/api.ts
npx snapback4 dev        # serves http://127.0.0.1:4400 and reloads on save
npm run dev              # in a second terminal: http://localhost:5173
```

`snapback4 init --web --port 4401` points the client at that backend and serves
the page on the next port (4402); `--web-port N` picks the page's port, which
`vite.config.mts` holds for both `npm run dev` and `vite preview`.

Choose "Continue as a guest", then add a few todos. Open a second tab to see
live updates; stop the backend to see writes queue offline and settle when it
returns.

## Files

- `snapback/schema.q`: the backend (tables, rules, `sync`, queries, mutations).
  Edit it and `snapback4 dev` re-adopts it; `snapback4 check` regenerates the API.
- `fixtures/`: seed rows and expected outcomes for `snapback4 test`.
- `src/client.ts`: one `createApp` for the whole app. This synced todo schema
  uses `device: webDevice()`; a bare `createClient()` in a browser refuses
  mutations with `E_ADMISSION`.
- `src/App.tsx`: sign-in, the live list (`usePage`) and writes (`useMutation`).
  `usePage` passes the query's cursor itself: a paged query takes `c: cursor ?`
  and reads `after c` (`npx snapback4 guide reads`); any other query reads
  with `useQuery`. Props name hook results by operation (`UsePage<typeof
  api.list>`, `UseQuery<…>`, `UseMutation<…>`), and `useSignedInViewer()` is
  the viewer below the sign-in. After you replace the schema,
  `npx snapback4 init --adapt` swaps this screen for a `Home` that compiles
  against any schema, while it is unmodified.
- `src/styles.css`, `index.html` (titled after this directory) and
  `vite.config.mts`: yours to change; `.mts` keeps Vite's config ESM even when
  package.json says `"type": "commonjs"`.

## Next steps

- Grow the schema: `npx snapback4 guide language`, then `guide rules`, `guide sync`.
- Try operations from the terminal: `npx snapback4 run add '{"title":"milk"}' --as alice`.
- Test without a server: `npx snapback4 test --fixture fixtures --as alice` loads
  `fixtures/todos.jsonl` and runs the expectations in `fixtures/test.cases.jsonl`
  (`npx snapback4 guide testing`).
- Add email accounts: change `use identity` to `use identity password` and see `guide auth`.
- Ship: `VITE_SNAPBACK_URL=https://your-backend npm run build` builds `dist/`
  with an offline app shell (`snapback4 web-shell dist`); serve it over HTTPS.
- A complete multi-screen example: `npx snapback4 init <dir> --web --template chat`.
# Todos: a Snapback 4 Expo starter

An Expo (React Native) app over a local-first Snapback 4 backend: one table,
one live query and three mutations. Development and production builds open the
native device and work offline; Expo Go uses the online client; web uses the
wasm device.

> **`npm install` takes a few minutes and reports about 15 high-severity
> advisories. They are expected; do not run `npm audit fix --force`** (it
> "fixes" them by downgrading Expo to SDK 44). All come from two packages in
> Expo's development toolchain with no patched release; neither is in the app
> bundle. Details under [Audit findings](#audit-findings).

```sh
npm install
npx snapback4 check      # compiles snapback/schema.q, writes snapback/generated/api.ts
npx snapback4 dev        # serves http://127.0.0.1:4400 and reloads on save
npm start                # in a second terminal: Expo Go, or press w for web
npm run typecheck
```

`npm run ios:dev` builds the native development build. The iOS simulator
reaches http://127.0.0.1:4400 on this machine (`init --expo --port N` writes
that port; `EXPO_PUBLIC_SNAPBACK_URL` overrides it); for a physical phone set
`EXPO_PUBLIC_SNAPBACK_URL` to your TLS deployment. Choose "Continue as a
guest", then add a few todos.

## Files

- `snapback/schema.q`: the backend (tables, rules, `sync`, queries, mutations).
  Edit it and `snapback4 dev` re-adopts it; `snapback4 check` regenerates the API.
- `fixtures/`: seed rows and expected outcomes for `snapback4 test`.
- `src/client.ts`: one `createApp` for the whole app; it picks the native
  device, the online client (Expo Go) or `webDevice()` (web).
- `App.tsx`: sign-in, the live list (`usePage`) and writes (`useMutation`).
  `usePage` passes the query's cursor itself: a paged query takes `c: cursor ?`
  and reads `after c` (`npx snapback4 guide reads`); any other query reads
  with `useQuery`. Props name hook results by operation (`UsePage<typeof
  api.list>`, `UseQuery<…>`, `UseMutation<…>`), and `useSignedInViewer()` is
  the viewer below the sign-in. After you replace the schema,
  `npx snapback4 init --adapt` swaps this screen for a `Home` that compiles
  against any schema, while it is unmodified.
- `expo-env.d.ts`: Expo's types, including `process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_*`.
- `metro.config.js` and `metro-wasm-transformer.cjs`: keep both; Metro then
  ships the web device's wasm and skips the local `.snapback4/` store.

Keep the Expo and `babel-preset-expo` major versions together; mixing them can
fail Hermes compilation.

## Audit findings

`package.json`'s `overrides` moves the config plugins' `xcode` from the
deprecated `uuid@7` to `uuid@11` (GHSA-w5hq-g745-h8pq). `npm audit` still
reports about 15 high findings from two advisories in Expo's development
toolchain, with no patched release of either package in any version:
`braces` (GHSA-vfj7-8cjw-p6xm, a stack-exhaustion denial of service on deeply
nested glob patterns; Metro's file watcher through `micromatch`) and
`node-forge` (GHSA-86w9-cpqp-85rv, RSA signature verification; the Expo CLI's
code-signing for updates). Neither is part of the app bundle, and the starter
passes neither untrusted patterns nor signatures to them. Do not run
`npm audit fix --force`: it "fixes" them by downgrading Expo to SDK 44.
`npx expo install --fix` takes Expo 57 patch releases as they ship.

## Native proof

`npm run export:ios` publishes Hermes to `dist/` and returns at every milestone;
native proof then runs in the background at background QoS, so you can keep
building. The next export and `npx snapback4 doctor` print its result; for a
final report run `npx snapback4 witness status --wait`. Receipts and logs stay
in `artifacts/native-smoke/<timestamp>`. `npm run native:smoke` explicitly
waits for a full Release build and smoke. Smoke proves the store opens,
acquisition, cold offline reopen and reconnect; pending write replay needs an
app scenario (`npx snapback4 guide witness`).

## Next steps

- Grow the schema: `npx snapback4 guide language`, then `guide rules`, `guide sync`.
- Try operations from the terminal: `npx snapback4 run add '{"title":"milk"}' --as alice`.
- Test without a server: `npx snapback4 test --fixture fixtures --as alice` loads
  `fixtures/todos.jsonl` and runs the expectations in `fixtures/test.cases.jsonl`
  (`npx snapback4 guide testing`).
- Add email accounts: change `use identity` to `use identity password` and see `guide auth`.
- A complete multi-screen example: `npx snapback4 init <dir> --expo --template chat`.

//! Validate the complete command before any project, store or host work.
const SWITCHES: &[&str] = &["list", "help", "initialize", "environment-skip", "background", "if-present", "check", "engine-info", "json", "json-objects", "trace-empty", "latency", "reset", "values", "no-watch", "fresh", "memory", "replay", "hold-effects", "hold-schedules", "resume-clock", "cost", "residency", "fix", "verbose", "online", "expo", "web", "allow-destructive", "restore-dormant", "allow-feed-adoption", "i-understand-cleartext", "skip-existing", "sections", "adapt", "dry-run", "apply", "full", "smoke", "prime", "relaunch", "restart", "wait"];

fn allowed(verb: &str) -> &'static str {
    match verb {
        "init" | "new" => "online expo web template recipe port port-range web-port skip-existing adapt dry-run apply prefix authors edges public-profiles public-posts public-follows public-reposts",
        "check" => "cost residency fix json verbose store fixture as emit-lean url release",
        "device" => "engine-info",
        "types" => "json",
        "dev" => "port bind no-watch fresh memory replay hold-effects hold-schedules clock-offset resume-clock trace-empty verbose i-understand-cleartext long-waits long-waits-per-principal claim-waits",
        "deploy" => "schema check verbose json restore-dormant allow-feed-adoption allow-destructive",
        "rollback" => "verbose json check to allow-feed-adoption allow-destructive",
        "prune" => "allow-destructive json",
        "releases" => "after items-after event json",
        "serve" => "clock-offset resume-clock now replay port bind worker i-understand-cleartext long-waits long-waits-per-principal claim-waits",
        "run" => "as port now json release token-env",
        "clock" => "port release",
        "reset" => "port seed clock release",
        "test" => "source fixture as residency verbose json",
        "why" => "verbose release values json instance store",
        "guide" => "list full sections section find",
        "data" => "release limit json",
        "sql" => "release limit json json-objects",
        "token" => "service days release json",
        "doctor" => "url latency reset json instance release",
        "upgrade" => "to json",
        "witness" => "email password-env mutation initialize environment-skip background if-present as url token restart runs query args artifacts timeout json build export smoke udid port bundle-id segment prime relaunch wait repack app-project field value button port-range",
        _ => "",
    }
}

pub fn validate(args: &[String]) -> Result<(), String> {
    let verb = args.first().map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("");
    // Pipe-owned private commands have positional protocols, not public flags.
    if matches!(verb, "--internal-witness-process" | "--internal-worker-watchdog") { return Ok(()); }
    let mut iter = args.iter().skip(1).peekable();
    while let Some(arg) = iter.next() {
        if arg == "--" { break; }
        if arg == "-h" { continue; }
        let Some(flag) = arg.strip_prefix("--") else {
            if arg.starts_with('-') && !arg[1..].starts_with(|c: char| c.is_ascii_digit()) {
                return Err(if verb == "witness" { "E_INPUT: witness has an invalid flag; see guide witness".into() } else { format!("E_INPUT: {verb} takes no {arg}") });
            }
            continue;
        };
        let (name, inline) = flag.split_once('=').map_or((flag, None), |(name, value)| (name, Some(value)));
        if name != "help" && !allowed(verb).split_whitespace().any(|known| known == name) {
            if verb == "rollback" && name == "dry-run" { return Err("E_INPUT: rollback preview is --check; use rollback <dir> [--to N|latest] --check".into()); }
            // Witness arguments may contain bearer material even in a flag name.
            return Err(if verb == "witness" { "E_INPUT: witness has an unknown flag; see guide witness".into() } else { format!("E_INPUT: {verb} takes no --{name}") });
        }
        if SWITCHES.contains(&name) {
            if inline.is_some() { return Err(format!("E_INPUT: {verb} --{name} takes no value")); }
        } else if inline.is_none() {
            // Retired spellings route to their existing no-work explanation,
            // even with a missing value; new unknown flags still refuse here.
            if matches!(verb, "init" | "new") && matches!(name, "recipe" | "prefix" | "authors" | "edges" | "public-profiles" | "public-posts" | "public-follows" | "public-reposts") {
                if iter.peek().is_some_and(|value| !value.starts_with("--")) { iter.next(); }
                continue;
            }
            if verb == "witness" && matches!(name, "repack" | "app-project") {
                if iter.peek().is_some_and(|value| !value.starts_with("--")) { iter.next(); }
                continue;
            }
            let Some(value) = iter.next() else { return Err(format!("E_INPUT: {verb} --{name} needs a value")); };
            // Search text is literal, including leading dashes and punctuation.
            if value.starts_with("--") && !matches!(name, "find" | "section") {
                return Err(format!("E_INPUT: {verb} --{name} needs a value"));
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub fn switch(name: &str) -> bool { SWITCHES.contains(&name) }

#[cfg(test)]
mod integration_tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn wave9_web_flags_and_upgrade_keep_shared_validation() {
        let args = ["witness", "web", "dist", "--url", "http://127.0.0.1", "--as", "alice", "--email", "alice@example.test", "--password-env", "PRIVATE_PASSWORD", "--mutation", "add", "--args", "{}", "--timeout", "30", "--export", "dist"].map(String::from);
        assert!(validate(&args).is_ok());
        for verb in ["upgrade", "witness"] {
            assert!(validate(&[verb.into(), "--unknown-wave9".into()]).is_err());
        }
        assert!(validate(&["upgrade".into(), "0.4.25".into()]).is_ok());
        assert!(validate(&["upgrade".into(), "--to".into(), "0.4.25".into(), "--json".into()]).is_ok());
        assert!(validate(&["witness".into(), "web".into(), "--password-env".into()]).is_err());
    }
}
